backend/services/uk/ons_service.py
"""
ONS API統合サービス
英国国家統計局データ取得
"""
import httpx
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List
from datetime import datetime
from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client
from backend.config import settings

class ONSService:
    """ONS API統合サービス"""
    
    BASE_URL = "https://api.ons.gov.uk/timeseries"
    
    DATASETS = {
        "cpi": "D7BT",  # CPI All Items Index
        "gdp": "IHYQ",  # GDP Quarterly
        "unemployment": "MGSX",  # Unemployment Rate
        "retail_sales": "J5EK"  # Retail Sales Index
    }
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0)
    
    async def fetch_cpi(
        self,
        start_date: Optional[str] = None,
        end_date: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """UK CPIを取得"""
        return await self._fetch_dataset(
            dataset_key="cpi",
            title="UK Consumer Price Index",
            unit="Index (2015=100)",
            start_date=start_date,
            end_date=end_date
        )
    
    async def fetch_gdp(
        self,
        start_date: Optional[str] = None,
        end_date: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """UK GDPを取得"""
        return await self._fetch_dataset(
            dataset_key="gdp",
            title="UK Gross Domestic Product",
            unit="GBP Million",
            start_date=start_date,
            end_date=end_date
        )
    
    async def fetch_unemployment(
        self,
        start_date: Optional[str] = None,
        end_date: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """UK失業率を取得"""
        return await self._fetch_dataset(
            dataset_key="unemployment",
            title="UK Unemployment Rate",
            unit="%",
            start_date=start_date,
            end_date=end_date
        )
    
    async def fetch_retail_sales(
        self,
        start_date: Optional[str] = None,
        end_date: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """UK小売売上高を取得"""
        return await self._fetch_dataset(
            dataset_key="retail_sales",
            title="UK Retail Sales Index",
            unit="Index (2019=100)",
            start_date=start_date,
            end_date=end_date
        )
    
    async def _fetch_dataset(
        self,
        dataset_key: str,
        title: str,
        unit: str,
        start_date: Optional[str] = None,
        end_date: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ONS APIからデータを取得（汎用メソッド）"""
        
        dataset_id = self.DATASETS[dataset_key]
        cache_key = f"ons:{dataset_id}:{start_date}:{end_date}"
        
        if cached := await redis_client.get(cache_key):
            return cached
        
        # APIリクエスト
        url = f"{self.BASE_URL}/{dataset_id}/data"
        
        try:
            response = await self.client.get(url)
            response.raise_for_status()
            
            json_data = response.json()
            
            # データパース
            data_points = self._parse_ons_response(json_data, start_date, end_date)
            
            result = {
                "dataset_id": dataset_id,
                "title": title,
                "unit": unit,
                "data": data_points,
                "last_updated": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "data_count": len(data_points)
            }
            
            # キャッシュ保存（24時間）
            await redis_client.set(cache_key, result, expire=86400)
            
            return result
            
        except httpx.HTTPError as e:
            print(f"Error fetching ONS dataset {dataset_id}: {e}")
            raise
    
    def _parse_ons_response(
        self,
        json_data: Dict[str, Any],
        start_date: Optional[str] = None,
        end_date: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ONS JSON レスポンスをパース"""
        data_points = []
        
        try:
            observations = json_data.get("observations", [])
            
            for obs in observations:
                date_str = obs.get("date")
                value = obs.get("value")
                
                # 日付フィルタリング
                if start_date and date_str < start_date:
                    continue
                if end_date and date_str > end_date:
                    continue
                
                try:
                    data_points.append({
                        "date": date_str,
                        "value": float(value)
                    })
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    continue
            
        except KeyError:
            pass
        
        return data_points
    
    async def close(self):
        """HTTPクライアントをクローズ"""
        await self.client.aclose()

# グローバルインスタンス
ons_service = ONSService()

"""
株式市場データAPI
yfinance、Alpha Vantageを使用
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import date, datetime
from pydantic import BaseModel

from backend.services.markets.yfinance_service import yfinance_service
from backend.services.markets.alphavantage_service import alphavantage_service
from backend.core.cache import cache_response

router = APIRouter(tags=["Markets - Stocks"])

# レスポンススキーマ
class StockOHLCV(BaseModel):
    date: str
    open: float
    high: float
    low: float
    close: float
    volume: int
    adjusted_close: Optional[float] = None

class StockDataResponse(BaseModel):
    symbol: str
    name: Optional[str] = None
    exchange: Optional[str] = None
    currency: Optional[str] = None
    data: List[StockOHLCV]
    last_updated: str
    data_count: int

class StockQuote(BaseModel):
    symbol: str
    price: float
    change: float
    change_percent: float
    volume: int
    market_cap: Optional[float] = None
    pe_ratio: Optional[float] = None
    timestamp: str

@router.get("/quote/{symbol}", response_model=StockQuote)
@cache_response(expire=60)  # 1分キャッシュ
async def get_stock_quote(symbol: str):
    """
    株式リアルタイム価格を取得
    
    例: AAPL (Apple), MSFT (Microsoft), 7203.T (トヨタ自動車)
    """
    try:
        quote = await yfinance_service.fetch_quote(symbol)
        
        if not quote:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Symbol {symbol} not found")
        
        return quote
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching quote: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/history/{symbol}", response_model=StockDataResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)  # 5分キャッシュ
async def get_stock_history(
    symbol: str,
    interval: str = Query("1d", regex="^(1m|5m|15m|30m|1h|1d|1wk|1mo)$"),
    period: str = Query("1y", regex="^(1d|5d|1mo|3mo|6mo|1y|2y|5y|10y|ytd|max)$")
):
    """
    株式ヒストリカルデータを取得
    
    interval: 1m, 5m, 15m, 30m, 1h, 1d, 1wk, 1mo
    period: 1d, 5d, 1mo, 3mo, 6mo, 1y, 2y, 5y, 10y, ytd, max
    """
    try:
        result = await yfinance_service.fetch_stock_data(
            symbol=symbol,
            interval=interval,
            period=period
        )
        
        if not result or result["data_count"] == 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No data found for {symbol}")
        
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching history: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/intraday/{symbol}", response_model=StockDataResponse)
@cache_response(expire=60)
async def get_stock_intraday(
    symbol: str,
    interval: str = Query("5min", regex="^(1min|5min|15min|30min|60min)$")
):
    """
    株式イントラデイデータを取得（Alpha Vantage使用）
    高頻度データが必要な場合に使用
    """
    try:
        result = await alphavantage_service.fetch_intraday(
            symbol=symbol,
            interval=interval
        )
        
        if not result or result["data_count"] == 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No data found for {symbol}")
        
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching intraday data: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/batch-quotes", response_model=List[StockQuote])
@cache_response(expire=60)
async def get_batch_quotes(
    symbols: str = Query(..., description="Comma-separated symbols (e.g., AAPL,MSFT,GOOGL)")
):
    """
    複数銘柄の株価を一括取得
    最大100銘柄まで
    """
    symbol_list = [s.strip() for s in symbols.split(",")]
    
    if len(symbol_list) > 100:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Maximum 100 symbols allowed")
    
    try:
        quotes = await yfinance_service.fetch_batch_quotes(symbol_list)
        return quotes
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching batch quotes: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/search")
async def search_stocks(
    query: str = Query(..., min_length=1, description="Search query (symbol or company name)")
):
    """
    銘柄検索
    シンボルまたは企業名で検索
    """
    try:
        results = await yfinance_service.search_symbols(query)
        return {"query": query, "results": results}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error searching stocks: {str(e)}")

"""
Alpha Vantage API統合サービス
高頻度株式データ、為替、商品データの取得
"""
import httpx
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List
from datetime import datetime
from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client
from backend.config import settings

class AlphaVantageService:
    """Alpha Vantage API統合サービス"""
    
    BASE_URL = "https://www.alphavantage.co/query"
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.api_key = settings.ALPHA_VANTAGE_API_KEY
        self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0)
    
    async def fetch_intraday(
        self,
        symbol: str,
        interval: str = "5min"
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """株式イントラデイデータを取得"""
        
        cache_key = f"alphavantage:intraday:{symbol}:{interval}"
        if cached := await redis_client.get(cache_key):
            return cached
        
        params = {
            "function": "TIME_SERIES_INTRADAY",
            "symbol": symbol,
            "interval": interval,
            "apikey": self.api_key,
            "outputsize": "full"
        }
        
        try:
            response = await self.client.get(self.BASE_URL, params=params)
            response.raise_for_status()
            
            json_data = response.json()
            
            # エラーチェック
            if "Error Message" in json_data:
                raise ValueError(f"Alpha Vantage Error: {json_data['Error Message']}")
            
            # データパース
            time_series_key = f"Time Series ({interval})"
            time_series = json_data.get(time_series_key, {})
            
            data_points = []
            for timestamp, values in time_series.items():
                data_points.append({
                    "date": timestamp,
                    "open": float(values["1. open"]),
                    "high": float(values["2. high"]),
                    "low": float(values["3. low"]),
                    "close": float(values["4. close"]),
                    "volume": int(values["5. volume"])
                })
            
            # 日付でソート（新しい順）
            data_points.sort(key=lambda x: x["date"], reverse=True)
            
            metadata = json_data.get("Meta Data", {})
            
            result = {
                "symbol": metadata.get("2. Symbol", symbol),
                "exchange": None,
                "currency": "USD",
                "data": data_points,
                "last_updated": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "data_count": len(data_points)
            }
            
            # キャッシュ保存（1分）
            await redis_client.set(cache_key, result, expire=60)
            
            return result
            
        except httpx.HTTPError as e:
            print(f"Error fetching Alpha Vantage intraday data for {symbol}: {e}")
            raise
    
    async def fetch_forex(
        self,
        from_currency: str,
        to_currency: str,
        interval: str = "5min"
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """為替データを取得"""
        
        cache_key = f"alphavantage:forex:{from_currency}_{to_currency}:{interval}"
        if cached := await redis_client.get(cache_key):
            return cached
        
        params = {
            "function": "FX_INTRADAY",
            "from_symbol": from_currency,
            "to_symbol": to_currency,
            "interval": interval,
            "apikey": self.api_key,
            "outputsize": "full"
        }
        
        try:
            response = await self.client.get(self.BASE_URL, params=params)
            response.raise_for_status()
            
            json_data = response.json()
            
            # データパース（実装は株式データと同様）
            time_series_key = f"Time Series FX ({interval})"
            time_series = json_data.get(time_series_key, {})
            
            data_points = []
            for timestamp, values in time_series.items():
                data_points.append({
                    "date": timestamp,
                    "open": float(values["1. open"]),
                    "high": float(values["2. high"]),
                    "low": float(values["3. low"]),
                    "close": float(values["4. close"])
                })
            
            data_points.sort(key=lambda x: x["date"], reverse=True)
            
            result = {
                "pair": f"{from_currency}/{to_currency}",
                "data": data_points,
                "last_updated": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "data_count": len(data_points)
            }
            
            await redis_client.set(cache_key, result, expire=60)
            
            return result
            
        except httpx.HTTPError as e:
            print(f"Error fetching forex data {from_currency}/{to_currency}: {e}")
            raise
    
    async def close(self):
        """HTTPクライアントをクローズ"""
        await self.client.aclose()

# グローバルインスタンス
alphavantage_service = AlphaVantageService()

"""
為替市場データAPI
Alpha Vantage、yfinanceを使用
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from pydantic import BaseModel

from backend.services.markets.alphavantage_service import alphavantage_service
from backend.services.markets.yfinance_service import yfinance_service
from backend.core.cache import cache_response

router = APIRouter(tags=["Markets - Forex"])

# レスポンススキーマ
class ForexOHLC(BaseModel):
    date: str
    open: float
    high: float
    low: float
    close: float

class ForexDataResponse(BaseModel):
    pair: str
    base_currency: str
    quote_currency: str
    data: List[ForexOHLC]
    last_updated: str
    data_count: int

class ForexQuote(BaseModel):
    pair: str
    rate: float
    bid: Optional[float] = None
    ask: Optional[float] = None
    change: Optional[float] = None
    change_percent: Optional[float] = None
    timestamp: str

@router.get("/quote/{base}/{quote}", response_model=ForexQuote)
@cache_response(expire=60)
async def get_forex_quote(
    base: str,
    quote: str
):
    """
    為替リアルタイムレートを取得
    
    例: 
    - /quote/USD/JPY -> USD/JPY レート
    - /quote/EUR/USD -> EUR/USD レート
    """
    try:
        quote_data = await yfinance_service.fetch_forex_quote(base, quote)
        
        if not quote_data:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Pair {base}/{quote} not found")
        
        return quote_data
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching forex quote: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/history/{base}/{quote}", response_model=ForexDataResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_forex_history(
    base: str,
    quote: str,
    interval: str = Query("1d", regex="^(1d|1wk|1mo)$"),
    period: str = Query("1y", regex="^(1mo|3mo|6mo|1y|2y|5y|max)$")
):
    """
    為替ヒストリカルデータを取得（日次・週次・月次）
    
    interval: 1d (日次), 1wk (週次), 1mo (月次)
    period: 1mo, 3mo, 6mo, 1y, 2y, 5y, max
    """
    try:
        result = await yfinance_service.fetch_forex_data(
            base=base,
            quote=quote,
            interval=interval,
            period=period
        )
        
        if not result or result["data_count"] == 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No data found for {base}/{quote}")
        
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching forex history: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/intraday/{base}/{quote}", response_model=ForexDataResponse)
@cache_response(expire=60)
async def get_forex_intraday(
    base: str,
    quote: str,
    interval: str = Query("5min", regex="^(1min|5min|15min|30min|60min)$")
):
    """
    為替イントラデイデータを取得（高頻度データ）
    Alpha Vantage使用
    
    interval: 1min, 5min, 15min, 30min, 60min
    """
    try:
        result = await alphavantage_service.fetch_forex(
            from_currency=base,
            to_currency=quote,
            interval=interval
        )
        
        if not result or result["data_count"] == 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No data found for {base}/{quote}")
        
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching forex intraday: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/major-pairs", response_model=List[ForexQuote])
@cache_response(expire=60)
async def get_major_pairs():
    """
    主要通貨ペアのレートを一括取得
    USD/JPY, EUR/USD, GBP/USD, USD/CHF, AUD/USD, USD/CAD, NZD/USD
    """
    major_pairs = [
        ("USD", "JPY"),
        ("EUR", "USD"),
        ("GBP", "USD"),
        ("USD", "CHF"),
        ("AUD", "USD"),
        ("USD", "CAD"),
        ("NZD", "USD")
    ]
    
    try:
        results = []
        for base, quote in major_pairs:
            quote_data = await yfinance_service.fetch_forex_quote(base, quote)
            if quote_data:
                results.append(quote_data)
        
        return results
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching major pairs: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/cross-rates/{base}")
@cache_response(expire=60)
async def get_cross_rates(
    base: str,
    quotes: str = Query("USD,EUR,JPY,GBP,CHF,AUD,CAD,NZD", description="Comma-separated quote currencies")
):
    """
    クロスレートを一括取得
    指定された基軸通貨に対する複数通貨のレート
    """
    quote_list = [q.strip() for q in quotes.split(",")]
    
    try:
        results = []
        for quote in quote_list:
            if quote == base:
                continue
            
            quote_data = await yfinance_service.fetch_forex_quote(base, quote)
            if quote_data:
                results.append(quote_data)
        
        return {
            "base_currency": base,
            "rates": results,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching cross rates: {str(e)}")

# 既存のyfinance_serviceに以下のメソッドを追加

async def fetch_forex_quote(self, base: str, quote: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """為替リアルタイムレートを取得"""
    
    # yfinanceでの為替シンボル形式: USDJPY=X
    symbol = f"{base}{quote}=X"
    cache_key = f"yfinance:forex_quote:{base}_{quote}"
    
    if cached := await redis_client.get(cache_key):
        return cached
    
    try:
        ticker = yf.Ticker(symbol)
        info = ticker.info
        history = ticker.history(period="2d")
        
        if history.empty:
            return None
        
        latest = history.iloc[-1]
        previous = history.iloc[-2] if len(history) > 1 else latest
        
        current_rate = float(latest['Close'])
        previous_rate = float(previous['Close'])
        change = current_rate - previous_rate
        change_percent = (change / previous_rate) * 100 if previous_rate != 0 else 0
        
        result = {
            "pair": f"{base}/{quote}",
            "rate": current_rate,
            "bid": info.get("bid"),
            "ask": info.get("ask"),
            "change": round(change, 5),
            "change_percent": round(change_percent, 2),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # キャッシュ保存（1分）
        await redis_client.set(cache_key, result, expire=60)
        
        return result
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error fetching forex quote for {base}/{quote}: {e}")
        return None

async def fetch_forex_data(
    self,
    base: str,
    quote: str,
    interval: str = "1d",
    period: str = "1y"
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """為替ヒストリカルデータを取得"""
    
    symbol = f"{base}{quote}=X"
    cache_key = f"yfinance:forex:{base}_{quote}:{interval}:{period}"
    
    if cached := await redis_client.get(cache_key):
        return cached
    
    try:
        ticker = yf.Ticker(symbol)
        df = ticker.history(period=period, interval=interval)
        
        if df.empty:
            return None
        
        data_points = []
        for index, row in df.iterrows():
            data_points.append({
                "date": index.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                "open": float(row['Open']),
                "high": float(row['High']),
                "low": float(row['Low']),
                "close": float(row['Close'])
            })
        
        result = {
            "pair": f"{base}/{quote}",
            "base_currency": base,
            "quote_currency": quote,
            "data": data_points,
            "last_updated": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "data_count": len(data_points)
        }
        
        # キャッシュ保存（5分）
        cache_ttl = 300 if interval in ["1m", "5m", "15m"] else 3600
        await redis_client.set(cache_key, result, expire=cache_ttl)
        
        return result
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error fetching forex data for {base}/{quote}: {e}")
        return None

"""
商品市場データAPI
金、原油、天然ガス、穀物など
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from pydantic import BaseModel

from backend.services.markets.yfinance_service import yfinance_service
from backend.core.cache import cache_response

router = APIRouter(tags=["Markets - Commodities"])

# レスポンススキーマ
class CommodityOHLCV(BaseModel):
    date: str
    open: float
    high: float
    low: float
    close: float
    volume: int

class CommodityDataResponse(BaseModel):
    symbol: str
    name: str
    unit: str
    data: List[CommodityOHLCV]
    last_updated: str
    data_count: int

class CommodityQuote(BaseModel):
    symbol: str
    name: str
    price: float
    change: float
    change_percent: float
    unit: str
    timestamp: str

# 商品シンボル定義
COMMODITY_SYMBOLS = {
    # エネルギー
    "crude_oil_wti": {"symbol": "CL=F", "name": "Crude Oil WTI", "unit": "USD/barrel"},
    "crude_oil_brent": {"symbol": "BZ=F", "name": "Crude Oil Brent", "unit": "USD/barrel"},
    "natural_gas": {"symbol": "NG=F", "name": "Natural Gas", "unit": "USD/MMBtu"},
    "heating_oil": {"symbol": "HO=F", "name": "Heating Oil", "unit": "USD/gallon"},
    "gasoline": {"symbol": "RB=F", "name": "Gasoline", "unit": "USD/gallon"},
    
    # 貴金属
    "gold": {"symbol": "GC=F", "name": "Gold", "unit": "USD/oz"},
    "silver": {"symbol": "SI=F", "name": "Silver", "unit": "USD/oz"},
    "platinum": {"symbol": "PL=F", "name": "Platinum", "unit": "USD/oz"},
    "palladium": {"symbol": "PA=F", "name": "Palladium", "unit": "USD/oz"},
    "copper": {"symbol": "HG=F", "name": "Copper", "unit": "USD/lb"},
    
    # 農産物
    "corn": {"symbol": "ZC=F", "name": "Corn", "unit": "USD/bushel"},
    "wheat": {"symbol": "ZW=F", "name": "Wheat", "unit": "USD/bushel"},
    "soybeans": {"symbol": "ZS=F", "name": "Soybeans", "unit": "USD/bushel"},
    "sugar": {"symbol": "SB=F", "name": "Sugar", "unit": "USD/lb"},
    "coffee": {"symbol": "KC=F", "name": "Coffee", "unit": "USD/lb"},
    "cotton": {"symbol": "CT=F", "name": "Cotton", "unit": "USD/lb"},
    "cocoa": {"symbol": "CC=F", "name": "Cocoa", "unit": "USD/ton"},
    
    # 畜産
    "live_cattle": {"symbol": "LE=F", "name": "Live Cattle", "unit": "USD/lb"},
    "lean_hogs": {"symbol": "HE=F", "name": "Lean Hogs", "unit": "USD/lb"}
}

@router.get("/quote/{commodity_id}", response_model=CommodityQuote)
@cache_response(expire=60)
async def get_commodity_quote(commodity_id: str):
    """
    商品リアルタイム価格を取得
    
    commodity_id: crude_oil_wti, gold, silver, corn, など
    """
    if commodity_id not in COMMODITY_SYMBOLS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Commodity '{commodity_id}' not found")
    
    commodity_info = COMMODITY_SYMBOLS[commodity_id]
    
    try:
        quote = await yfinance_service.fetch_quote(commodity_info["symbol"])
        
        if not quote:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No data for {commodity_id}")
        
        return {
            "symbol": commodity_id,
            "name": commodity_info["name"],
            "price": quote["price"],
            "change": quote["change"],
            "change_percent": quote["change_percent"],
            "unit": commodity_info["unit"],
            "timestamp": quote["timestamp"]
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching commodity quote: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/history/{commodity_id}", response_model=CommodityDataResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_commodity_history(
    commodity_id: str,
    interval: str = Query("1d", regex="^(1d|1wk|1mo)$"),
    period: str = Query("1y", regex="^(1mo|3mo|6mo|1y|2y|5y|10y|max)$")
):
    """
    商品ヒストリカルデータを取得
    
    interval: 1d, 1wk, 1mo
    period: 1mo, 3mo, 6mo, 1y, 2y, 5y, 10y, max
    """
    if commodity_id not in COMMODITY_SYMBOLS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Commodity '{commodity_id}' not found")
    
    commodity_info = COMMODITY_SYMBOLS[commodity_id]
    
    try:
        result = await yfinance_service.fetch_stock_data(
            symbol=commodity_info["symbol"],
            interval=interval,
            period=period
        )
        
        if not result or result["data_count"] == 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No data for {commodity_id}")
        
        return {
            "symbol": commodity_id,
            "name": commodity_info["name"],
            "unit": commodity_info["unit"],
            "data": result["data"],
            "last_updated": result["last_updated"],
            "data_count": result["data_count"]
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching commodity history: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/list")
async def list_all_commodities():
    """利用可能な全商品のリストを取得"""
    commodities_by_category = {
        "energy": [],
        "metals": [],
        "agriculture": [],
        "livestock": []
    }
    
    for commodity_id, info in COMMODITY_SYMBOLS.items():
        item = {
            "id": commodity_id,
            "name": info["name"],
            "symbol": info["symbol"],
            "unit": info["unit"]
        }
        
        if "oil" in commodity_id or "gas" in commodity_id or "heating" in commodity_id or "gasoline" in commodity_id:
            commodities_by_category["energy"].append(item)
        elif commodity_id in ["gold", "silver", "platinum", "palladium", "copper"]:
            commodities_by_category["metals"].append(item)
        elif "cattle" in commodity_id or "hogs" in commodity_id:
            commodities_by_category["livestock"].append(item)
        else:
            commodities_by_category["agriculture"].append(item)
    
    return commodities_by_category

"""
経済指標カレンダーAPI
各国の経済指標発表スケジュールと実績データ
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy import select, and_

from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.calendar import EconomicCalendar
from backend.services.features.calendar_service import calendar_service
from backend.core.cache import cache_response

router = APIRouter(tags=["Features - Calendar"])

# レスポンススキーマ
class CalendarEvent(BaseModel):
    id: int
    country: str
    indicator_name: str
    release_date: datetime
    period: str
    importance: str  # high, medium, low
    forecast: Optional[float] = None
    previous: Optional[float] = None
    actual: Optional[float] = None
    unit: Optional[str] = None
    source: Optional[str] = None
    beat_miss: Optional[str] = None  # beat, miss, inline
    surprise_index: Optional[float] = None

class CalendarResponse(BaseModel):
    events: List[CalendarEvent]
    date_range: dict
    total_count: int

@router.get("/events", response_model=CalendarResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_calendar_events(
    start_date: Optional[date] = Query(None, description="Start date (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
    end_date: Optional[date] = Query(None, description="End date (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Country code (US, JP, UK, etc.)"),
    importance: Optional[str] = Query(None, regex="^(high|medium|low)$"),
    limit: int = Query(100, le=500)
):
    """
    経済指標カレンダーイベントを取得
    
    デフォルトで今日から7日間のイベントを返す
    """
    if not start_date:
        start_date = date.today()
    if not end_date:
        end_date = start_date + timedelta(days=7)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(EconomicCalendar).where(
                and_(
                    EconomicCalendar.release_date >= start_date,
                    EconomicCalendar.release_date <= end_date
                )
            )
            
            if country:
                query = query.where(EconomicCalendar.country == country.upper())
            
            if importance:
                query = query.where(EconomicCalendar.importance == importance)
            
            query = query.order_by(EconomicCalendar.release_date).limit(limit)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            events = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "events": events,
                "date_range": {
                    "start": start_date.isoformat(),
                    "end": end_date.isoformat()
                },
                "total_count": len(events)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching calendar events: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/today", response_model=CalendarResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_today_events(
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    importance: Optional[str] = Query(None)
):
    """今日の経済指標イベントを取得"""
    today = date.today()
    return await get_calendar_events(
        start_date=today,
        end_date=today,
        country=country,
        importance=importance
    )

@router.get("/this-week", response_model=CalendarResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_this_week_events(
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    importance: Optional[str] = Query(None)
):
    """今週の経済指標イベントを取得"""
    today = date.today()
    start_of_week = today - timedelta(days=today.weekday())
    end_of_week = start_of_week + timedelta(days=6)
    
    return await get_calendar_events(
        start_date=start_of_week,
        end_date=end_of_week,
        country=country,
        importance=importance
    )

@router.get("/high-impact", response_model=CalendarResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_high_impact_events(
    start_date: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    end_date: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None)
):
    """高重要度の経済指標イベントのみを取得"""
    if not start_date:
        start_date = date.today()
    if not end_date:
        end_date = start_date + timedelta(days=30)
    
    return await get_calendar_events(
        start_date=start_date,
        end_date=end_date,
        country=country,
        importance="high"
    )

@router.get("/surprises", response_model=CalendarResponse)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_recent_surprises(
    days: int = Query(7, ge=1, le=90),
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    min_surprise: float = Query(0.5, description="Minimum surprise index (0-1)")
):
    """
    最近のサプライズ（予想と実績の乖離が大きい）イベントを取得
    """
    start_date = date.today() - timedelta(days=days)
    end_date = date.today()
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(EconomicCalendar).where(
                and_(
                    EconomicCalendar.release_date >= start_date,
                    EconomicCalendar.release_date <= end_date,
                    EconomicCalendar.actual.isnot(None),
                    EconomicCalendar.surprise_index >= min_surprise
                )
            )
            
            if country:
                query = query.where(EconomicCalendar.country == country.upper())
            
            query = query.order_by(EconomicCalendar.surprise_index.desc()).limit(50)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            events = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "events": events,
                "date_range": {
                    "start": start_date.isoformat(),
                    "end": end_date.isoformat()
                },
                "total_count": len(events)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching surprises: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/refresh")
async def refresh_calendar():
    """
    カレンダーデータを手動更新
    （管理者用エンドポイント）
    """
    try:
        result = await calendar_service.fetch_and_update_calendar()
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "updated_count": result["updated_count"],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error refreshing calendar: {str(e)}")

"""
経済指標カレンダーサービス
Trading Economics、Investing.comなどからデータ取得
"""
import httpx
from typing import Dict, Any, List
from datetime import datetime, date, timedelta
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client
from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.calendar import EconomicCalendar
from backend.config import settings

class CalendarService:
    """経済指標カレンダーサービス"""
    
    # Trading Economics API
    TE_BASE_URL = "https://api.tradingeconomics.com"
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.te_api_key = settings.TRADING_ECONOMICS_API_KEY
        self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0)
    
    async def fetch_and_update_calendar(
        self,
        start_date: Optional[date] = None,
        end_date: Optional[date] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        経済指標カレンダーを取得してデータベースを更新
        """
        if not start_date:
            start_date = date.today()
        if not end_date:
            end_date = start_date + timedelta(days=30)
        
        # Trading Economics APIから取得
        events = await self._fetch_from_trading_economics(start_date, end_date)
        
        # データベースに保存
        updated_count = await self._save_events_to_db(events)
        
        return {
            "updated_count": updated_count,
            "date_range": {
                "start": start_date.isoformat(),
                "end": end_date.isoformat()
            }
        }
    
    async def _fetch_from_trading_economics(
        self,
        start_date: date,
        end_date: date
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Trading Economics APIからカレンダーデータを取得"""
        
        url = f"{self.TE_BASE_URL}/calendar"
        params = {
            "c": self.te_api_key,
            "d1": start_date.isoformat(),
            "d2": end_date.isoformat(),
            "f": "json"
        }
        
        try:
            response = await self.client.get(url, params=params)
            response.raise_for_status()
            
            data = response.json()
            
            events = []
            for item in data:
                # サプライズインデックスを計算
                surprise_index = self._calculate_surprise_index(
                    forecast=item.get("Forecast"),
                    actual=item.get("Actual"),
                    previous=item.get("Previous")
                )
                
                # Beat/Miss判定
                beat_miss = self._determine_beat_miss(
                    forecast=item.get("Forecast"),
                    actual=item.get("Actual")
                )
                
                events.append({
                    "country": item.get("Country"),
                    "indicator_name": item.get("Event"),
                    "release_date": datetime.fromisoformat(item.get("Date")),
                    "period": item.get("Reference"),
                    "importance": self._map_importance(item.get("Importance")),
                    "forecast": item.get("Forecast"),
                    "previous": item.get("Previous"),
                    "actual": item.get("Actual"),
                    "unit": item.get("Unit"),
                    "source": "Trading Economics",
                    "beat_miss": beat_miss,
                    "surprise_index": surprise_index
                })
            
            return events
            
        except httpx.HTTPError as e:
            print(f"Error fetching calendar from Trading Economics: {e}")
            return []
    
    def _calculate_surprise_index(
        self,
        forecast: Optional[float],
        actual: Optional[float],
        previous: Optional[float]
    ) -> Optional[float]:
        """
        サプライズインデックスを計算
        予想と実績の乖離度を0-1でスコア化
        """
        if not forecast or not actual:
            return None
        
        try:
            if forecast == 0:
                return None
            
            surprise = abs((actual - forecast) / forecast)
            # 0-1にスケール（100%以上の乖離は1.0）
            return min(surprise, 1.0)
            
        except (TypeError, ZeroDivisionError):
            return None
    
    def _determine_beat_miss(
        self,
        forecast: Optional[float],
        actual: Optional[float]
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        """Beat/Miss判定"""
        if not forecast or not actual:
            return None
        
        diff_percent = abs((actual - forecast) / forecast) * 100 if forecast != 0 else 0
        
        if diff_percent < 1:  # 1%未満の差は inline
            return "inline"
        elif actual > forecast:
            return "beat"
        else:
            return "miss"
    
    def _map_importance(self, importance: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """重要度をマッピング"""
        if not importance:
            return "medium"
        
        importance = str(importance).lower()
        
        if importance in ["3", "high", "***"]:
            return "high"
        elif importance in ["2", "medium", "**"]:
            return "medium"
        else:
            return "low"
    
    async def _save_events_to_db(self, events: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> int:
        """イベントをデータベースに保存"""
        # 実装: SQLAlchemyでのINSERT/UPDATE処理
        # 重複チェック（country + indicator_name + release_date）
        updated_count = 0
        
        async with get_db() as db:
            for event in events:
                # UPSERT処理
                # 実装省略
                updated_count += 1
        
        return updated_count
    
    async def close(self):
        """HTTPクライアントをクローズ"""
        await self.client.aclose()

# グローバルインスタンス
calendar_service = CalendarService()

"""
決算カレンダーAPI
米国・日本企業の決算発表スケジュールと実績データ
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy import select, and_

from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.calendar import EarningsCalendar
from backend.services.features.earnings_service import earnings_service
from backend.core.cache import cache_response

router = APIRouter(tags=["Features - Earnings"])

# レスポンススキーマ
class EarningsEvent(BaseModel):
    id: int
    symbol: str
    company_name: str
    country: str
    report_date: datetime
    fiscal_quarter: str
    fiscal_year: int
    
    # 予想値
    eps_estimate: Optional[float] = None
    revenue_estimate: Optional[float] = None
    
    # 実績値
    eps_actual: Optional[float] = None
    revenue_actual: Optional[float] = None
    
    # サプライズ
    eps_surprise: Optional[float] = None
    eps_surprise_percent: Optional[float] = None
    revenue_surprise: Optional[float] = None
    revenue_surprise_percent: Optional[float] = None
    
    # その他
    currency: str
    market_cap: Optional[float] = None
    sector: Optional[str] = None
    industry: Optional[str] = None

class EarningsResponse(BaseModel):
    events: List[EarningsEvent]
    date_range: dict
    total_count: int

@router.get("/calendar", response_model=EarningsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_earnings_calendar(
    start_date: Optional[date] = Query(None, description="Start date (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
    end_date: Optional[date] = Query(None, description="End date (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Country code (US, JP)"),
    sector: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Sector name"),
    limit: int = Query(100, le=500)
):
    """
    決算カレンダーを取得
    
    デフォルトで今日から14日間の決算を返す
    """
    if not start_date:
        start_date = date.today()
    if not end_date:
        end_date = start_date + timedelta(days=14)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(EarningsCalendar).where(
                and_(
                    EarningsCalendar.report_date >= start_date,
                    EarningsCalendar.report_date <= end_date
                )
            )
            
            if country:
                query = query.where(EarningsCalendar.country == country.upper())
            
            if sector:
                query = query.where(EarningsCalendar.sector == sector)
            
            query = query.order_by(EarningsCalendar.report_date).limit(limit)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            events = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "events": events,
                "date_range": {
                    "start": start_date.isoformat(),
                    "end": end_date.isoformat()
                },
                "total_count": len(events)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching earnings calendar: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/today", response_model=EarningsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_today_earnings(
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    sector: Optional[str] = Query(None)
):
    """今日の決算発表を取得"""
    today = date.today()
    return await get_earnings_calendar(
        start_date=today,
        end_date=today,
        country=country,
        sector=sector
    )

@router.get("/this-week", response_model=EarningsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_this_week_earnings(
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    sector: Optional[str] = Query(None)
):
    """今週の決算発表を取得"""
    today = date.today()
    start_of_week = today - timedelta(days=today.weekday())
    end_of_week = start_of_week + timedelta(days=6)
    
    return await get_earnings_calendar(
        start_date=start_of_week,
        end_date=end_of_week,
        country=country,
        sector=sector
    )

@router.get("/symbol/{symbol}", response_model=EarningsEvent)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_symbol_next_earnings(symbol: str):
    """
    特定銘柄の次回決算情報を取得
    """
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(EarningsCalendar).where(
                and_(
                    EarningsCalendar.symbol == symbol.upper(),
                    EarningsCalendar.report_date >= date.today()
                )
            ).order_by(EarningsCalendar.report_date).limit(1)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            event = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            
            if not event:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No upcoming earnings for {symbol}")
            
            return event
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching earnings: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/surprises", response_model=EarningsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_earnings_surprises(
    days: int = Query(7, ge=1, le=90),
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    min_surprise_percent: float = Query(5.0, description="Minimum EPS surprise percent")
):
    """
    最近のEPSサプライズ（予想との大きな乖離）を取得
    """
    start_date = date.today() - timedelta(days=days)
    end_date = date.today()
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(EarningsCalendar).where(
                and_(
                    EarningsCalendar.report_date >= start_date,
                    EarningsCalendar.report_date <= end_date,
                    EarningsCalendar.eps_actual.isnot(None),
                    EarningsCalendar.eps_surprise_percent >= min_surprise_percent
                )
            )
            
            if country:
                query = query.where(EarningsCalendar.country == country.upper())
            
            query = query.order_by(EarningsCalendar.eps_surprise_percent.desc()).limit(50)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            events = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "events": events,
                "date_range": {
                    "start": start_date.isoformat(),
                    "end": end_date.isoformat()
                },
                "total_count": len(events)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching surprises: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/mega-cap", response_model=EarningsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_mega_cap_earnings(
    start_date: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    end_date: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    min_market_cap: float = Query(100.0, description="Minimum market cap in billions")
):
    """
    大型株の決算カレンダーを取得
    """
    if not start_date:
        start_date = date.today()
    if not end_date:
        end_date = start_date + timedelta(days=30)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(EarningsCalendar).where(
                and_(
                    EarningsCalendar.report_date >= start_date,
                    EarningsCalendar.report_date <= end_date,
                    EarningsCalendar.market_cap >= min_market_cap * 1_000_000_000  # Convert to dollars
                )
            ).order_by(EarningsCalendar.market_cap.desc()).limit(100)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            events = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "events": events,
                "date_range": {
                    "start": start_date.isoformat(),
                    "end": end_date.isoformat()
                },
                "total_count": len(events)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching mega cap earnings: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/refresh")
async def refresh_earnings_calendar():
    """
    決算カレンダーデータを手動更新
    （管理者用エンドポイント）
    """
    try:
        result = await earnings_service.fetch_and_update_earnings()
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "updated_count": result["updated_count"],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error refreshing earnings: {str(e)}")

backend/services/features/earnings_service.py
"""
決算カレンダーサービス
Alpha Vantage、Yahoo Finance、日本株はEDINETなどから取得
"""
import httpx
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, date, timedelta
import yfinance as yf

from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client
from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.calendar import EarningsCalendar
from backend.config import settings

class EarningsService:
    """決算カレンダーサービス"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.alpha_vantage_key = settings.ALPHA_VANTAGE_API_KEY
        self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0)
    
    async def fetch_and_update_earnings(
        self,
        start_date: Optional[date] = None,
        end_date: Optional[date] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        決算カレンダーを取得してデータベースを更新
        """
        if not start_date:
            start_date = date.today()
        if not end_date:
            end_date = start_date + timedelta(days=30)
        
        # 米国株の決算を取得
        us_earnings = await self._fetch_us_earnings(start_date, end_date)
        
        # 日本株の決算を取得
        jp_earnings = await self._fetch_jp_earnings(start_date, end_date)
        
        # データベースに保存
        all_earnings = us_earnings + jp_earnings
        updated_count = await self._save_earnings_to_db(all_earnings)
        
        return {
            "updated_count": updated_count,
            "date_range": {
                "start": start_date.isoformat(),
                "end": end_date.isoformat()
            }
        }
    
    async def _fetch_us_earnings(
        self,
        start_date: date,
        end_date: date
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """米国株の決算情報を取得（Alpha Vantage使用）"""
        
        earnings = []
        
        # 主要米国株のリスト（例: S&P 100）
        # 実際にはデータベースから取得
        major_symbols = [
            "AAPL", "MSFT", "GOOGL", "AMZN", "NVDA", "META", "TSLA", "BRK.B",
            "V", "JNJ", "WMT", "JPM", "MA", "PG", "UNH", "HD", "DIS", "BAC",
            "XOM", "ORCL", "KO", "PFE", "ABBV", "CVX", "CSCO", "PEP", "MRK"
        ]
        
        for symbol in major_symbols:
            try:
                earnings_data = await self._fetch_symbol_earnings(symbol, "US")
                if earnings_data:
                    earnings.extend(earnings_data)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error fetching earnings for {symbol}: {e}")
                continue
        
        return earnings
    
    async def _fetch_jp_earnings(
        self,
        start_date: date,
        end_date: date
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """日本株の決算情報を取得"""
        
        earnings = []
        
        # 主要日本株のリスト（日経225主要銘柄）
        major_symbols = [
            "7203.T",  # トヨタ自動車
            "6758.T",  # ソニー
            "9984.T",  # ソフトバンクG
            "6861.T",  # キーエンス
            "8306.T",  # 三菱UFJ
            "9432.T",  # NTT
            "7974.T",  # 任天堂
            "6902.T",  # デンソー
            "8316.T",  # 三井住友FG
            "6981.T",  # 村田製作所
        ]
        
        for symbol in major_symbols:
            try:
                earnings_data = await self._fetch_symbol_earnings(symbol, "JP")
                if earnings_data:
                    earnings.extend(earnings_data)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error fetching earnings for {symbol}: {e}")
                continue
        
        return earnings
    
    async def _fetch_symbol_earnings(
        self,
        symbol: str,
        country: str
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """個別銘柄の決算情報を取得"""
        
        cache_key = f"earnings:{symbol}"
        if cached := await redis_client.get(cache_key):
            return cached
        
        try:
            # yfinanceで決算情報を取得
            ticker = yf.Ticker(symbol)
            
            # 次回決算日
            earnings_dates = ticker.earnings_dates
            calendar = ticker.calendar
            
            if calendar is None or earnings_dates is None:
                return []
            
            # 会社情報
            info = ticker.info
            
            earnings_data = []
            
            # 次回決算情報
            if 'Earnings Date' in calendar:
                earnings_date = calendar['Earnings Date'][0]
                
                # 予想EPS・売上
                eps_estimate = calendar.get('Earnings Average', None)
                revenue_estimate = calendar.get('Revenue Average', None)
                
                earnings_data.append({
                    "symbol": symbol,
                    "company_name": info.get("longName", symbol),
                    "country": country,
                    "report_date": earnings_date,
                    "fiscal_quarter": self._get_fiscal_quarter(earnings_date),
                    "fiscal_year": earnings_date.year,
                    "eps_estimate": eps_estimate,
                    "revenue_estimate": revenue_estimate,
                    "eps_actual": None,
                    "revenue_actual": None,
                    "currency": "USD" if country == "US" else "JPY",
                    "market_cap": info.get("marketCap"),
                    "sector": info.get("sector"),
                    "industry": info.get("industry")
                })
            
            # 過去の決算実績（直近4四半期）
            if not earnings_dates.empty:
                for idx, row in earnings_dates.head(4).iterrows():
                    eps_estimate = row.get('EPS Estimate')
                    eps_actual = row.get('Reported EPS')
                    
                    if eps_estimate and eps_actual:
                        eps_surprise = eps_actual - eps_estimate
                        eps_surprise_percent = (eps_surprise / abs(eps_estimate)) * 100 if eps_estimate != 0 else 0
                    else:
                        eps_surprise = None
                        eps_surprise_percent = None
                    
                    earnings_data.append({
                        "symbol": symbol,
                        "company_name": info.get("longName", symbol),
                        "country": country,
                        "report_date": idx,
                        "fiscal_quarter": self._get_fiscal_quarter(idx),
                        "fiscal_year": idx.year,
                        "eps_estimate": eps_estimate,
                        "revenue_estimate": None,
                        "eps_actual": eps_actual,
                        "revenue_actual": None,
                        "eps_surprise": eps_surprise,
                        "eps_surprise_percent": eps_surprise_percent,
                        "currency": "USD" if country == "US" else "JPY",
                        "market_cap": info.get("marketCap"),
                        "sector": info.get("sector"),
                        "industry": info.get("industry")
                    })
            
            # キャッシュ保存（6時間）
            await redis_client.set(cache_key, earnings_data, expire=21600)
            
            return earnings_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error fetching earnings for {symbol}: {e}")
            return []
    
    def _get_fiscal_quarter(self, date: datetime) -> str:
        """日付から会計四半期を取得"""
        quarter = (date.month - 1) // 3 + 1
        return f"Q{quarter}"
    
    async def _save_earnings_to_db(self, earnings: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> int:
        """決算データをデータベースに保存"""
        # 実装: SQLAlchemyでのINSERT/UPDATE処理
        # 重複チェック（symbol + report_date）
        updated_count = 0
        
        async with get_db() as db:
            for earning in earnings:
                # UPSERT処理
                # 実装省略
                updated_count += 1
        
        return updated_count
    
    async def close(self):
        """HTTPクライアントをクローズ"""
        await self.client.aclose()

# グローバルインスタンス
earnings_service = EarningsService()

backend/routers/features/news.py
"""
ニュース・ヘッドラインAPI
Twitter、金融ニュースサイトから取得
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy import select, and_, or_

from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.news import NewsHeadline
from backend.services.features.news_service import news_service
from backend.core.cache import cache_response

router = APIRouter(tags=["Features - News"])

# レスポンススキーマ
class NewsArticle(BaseModel):
    id: int
    title: str
    summary: Optional[str] = None
    url: str
    source: str
    author: Optional[str] = None
    published_at: datetime
    category: Optional[str] = None
    
    # タグ・エンティティ
    symbols: Optional[List[str]] = None
    countries: Optional[List[str]] = None
    keywords: Optional[List[str]] = None
    
    # センチメント
    sentiment: Optional[str] = None  # positive, neutral, negative
    sentiment_score: Optional[float] = None  # -1 to 1
    
    # エンゲージメント
    views: Optional[int] = None
    likes: Optional[int] = None
    shares: Optional[int] = None

class NewsResponse(BaseModel):
    articles: List[NewsArticle]
    total_count: int
    page: int
    page_size: int

@router.get("/headlines", response_model=NewsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_news_headlines(
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    source: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="News source (bloomberg, reuters, twitter, etc.)"),
    category: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Category (economy, markets, policy, etc.)"),
    country: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Country code"),
    symbol: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Stock symbol"),
    sentiment: Optional[str] = Query(None, regex="^(positive|neutral|negative)$"),
    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=168, description="Time range in hours")
):
    """
    ニュースヘッドラインを取得
    
    デフォルトで過去24時間のニュースを返す
    """
    start_time = datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=hours)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(NewsHeadline).where(
                NewsHeadline.published_at >= start_time
            )
            
            if source:
                query = query.where(NewsHeadline.source == source)
            
            if category:
                query = query.where(NewsHeadline.category == category)
            
            if country:
                query = query.where(NewsHeadline.countries.contains([country]))
            
            if symbol:
                query = query.where(NewsHeadline.symbols.contains([symbol]))
            
            if sentiment:
                query = query.where(NewsHeadline.sentiment == sentiment)
            
            # 総数取得
            count_query = select(db.func.count()).select_from(query.subquery())
            total_count = await db.scalar(count_query)
            
            # ページネーション
            offset = (page - 1) * page_size
            query = query.order_by(NewsHeadline.published_at.desc()).offset(offset).limit(page_size)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            articles = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "articles": articles,
                "total_count": total_count,
                "page": page,
                "page_size": page_size
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching news: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/trending", response_model=NewsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_trending_news(
    hours: int = Query(6, ge=1, le=48),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100)
):
    """
    トレンドニュースを取得（エンゲージメントが高い記事）
    """
    start_time = datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=hours)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            # エンゲージメントスコアでソート（views + likes*2 + shares*3）
            query = select(NewsHeadline).where(
                NewsHeadline.published_at >= start_time
            ).order_by(
                (NewsHeadline.views + 
                 NewsHeadline.likes * 2 + 
                 NewsHeadline.shares * 3).desc()
            ).limit(page_size)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            articles = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "articles": articles,
                "total_count": len(articles),
                "page": 1,
                "page_size": page_size
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching trending news: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/by-symbol/{symbol}", response_model=NewsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_news_by_symbol(
    symbol: str,
    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=168),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100)
):
    """特定銘柄に関連するニュースを取得"""
    start_time = datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=hours)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(NewsHeadline).where(
                and_(
                    NewsHeadline.published_at >= start_time,
                    NewsHeadline.symbols.contains([symbol.upper()])
                )
            ).order_by(NewsHeadline.published_at.desc()).limit(page_size)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            articles = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "articles": articles,
                "total_count": len(articles),
                "page": 1,
                "page_size": page_size
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching news for {symbol}: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/sentiment-summary/{symbol}")
@cache_response(expire=600)
async def get_sentiment_summary(
    symbol: str,
    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=168)
):
    """
    特定銘柄のセンチメントサマリーを取得
    ポジティブ・ニュートラル・ネガティブの記事数と平均スコア
    """
    start_time = datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=hours)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(NewsHeadline).where(
                and_(
                    NewsHeadline.published_at >= start_time,
                    NewsHeadline.symbols.contains([symbol.upper()]),
                    NewsHeadline.sentiment.isnot(None)
                )
            )
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            articles = result.scalars().all()
            
            # センチメント集計
            sentiment_counts = {"positive": 0, "neutral": 0, "negative": 0}
            sentiment_scores = []
            
            for article in articles:
                sentiment_counts[article.sentiment] += 1
                if article.sentiment_score is not None:
                    sentiment_scores.append(article.sentiment_score)
            
            avg_score = sum(sentiment_scores) / len(sentiment_scores) if sentiment_scores else 0
            
            return {
                "symbol": symbol.upper(),
                "time_range_hours": hours,
                "total_articles": len(articles),
                "sentiment_counts": sentiment_counts,
                "average_sentiment_score": round(avg_score, 3),
                "sentiment_distribution": {
                    "positive_percent": round(sentiment_counts["positive"] / len(articles) * 100, 1) if articles else 0,
                    "neutral_percent": round(sentiment_counts["neutral"] / len(articles) * 100, 1) if articles else 0,
                    "negative_percent": round(sentiment_counts["negative"] / len(articles) * 100, 1) if articles else 0
                }
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching sentiment: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/search")
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def search_news(
    q: str = Query(..., min_length=2, description="Search query"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100)
):
    """
    ニュースをキーワード検索
    タイトル・サマリー・キーワードから検索
    """
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            # 全文検索（PostgreSQL to_tsquery使用）
            query = select(NewsHeadline).where(
                or_(
                    NewsHeadline.title.ilike(f"%{q}%"),
                    NewsHeadline.summary.ilike(f"%{q}%"),
                    NewsHeadline.keywords.contains([q])
                )
            )
            
            # 総数取得
            count_query = select(db.func.count()).select_from(query.subquery())
            total_count = await db.scalar(count_query)
            
            # ページネーション
            offset = (page - 1) * page_size
            query = query.order_by(NewsHeadline.published_at.desc()).offset(offset).limit(page_size)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            articles = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "query": q,
                "articles": articles,
                "total_count": total_count,
                "page": page,
                "page_size": page_size
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error searching news: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/refresh")
async def refresh_news():
    """
    ニュースデータを手動更新
    （管理者用エンドポイント）
    """
    try:
        result = await news_service.fetch_and_update_news()
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "fetched_count": result["fetched_count"],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error refreshing news: {str(e)}")

"""
ニュースサービス
Twitter API、RSS、スクレイピングでニュース取得
"""
import httpx
import tweepy
import feedparser
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client
from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.news import NewsHeadline
from backend.config import settings

class NewsService:
    """ニュース取得サービス"""
    
    # RSSフィードURL
    RSS_FEEDS = {
        "bloomberg": "https://www.bloomberg.com/feed/podcast/etf-report.xml",
        "reuters": "https://www.reutersagency.com/feed/",
        "cnbc": "https://www.cnbc.com/id/100003114/device/rss/rss.html",
        "ft": "https://www.ft.com/rss/home",
    }
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0)
        
        # Twitter API v2クライアント
        if settings.TWITTER_BEARER_TOKEN:
            self.twitter_client = tweepy.Client(
                bearer_token=settings.TWITTER_BEARER_TOKEN
            )
        else:
            self.twitter_client = None
    
    async def fetch_and_update_news(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        各ソースからニュースを取得してデータベースを更新
        """
        all_articles = []
        
        # RSSフィードから取得
        rss_articles = await self._fetch_from_rss()
        all_articles.extend(rss_articles)
        
        # Twitterから取得
        if self.twitter_client:
            twitter_articles = await self._fetch_from_twitter()
            all_articles.extend(twitter_articles)
        
        # データベースに保存
        saved_count = await self._save_articles_to_db(all_articles)
        
        return {
            "fetched_count": saved_count,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _fetch_from_rss(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """RSSフィードからニュースを取得"""
        articles = []
        
        for source, feed_url in self.RSS_FEEDS.items():
            try:
                response = await self.client.get(feed_url)
                feed = feedparser.parse(response.text)
                
                for entry in feed.entries[:10]:  # 最新10件
                    published = entry.get('published_parsed')
                    if published:
                        published_dt = datetime(*published[:6])
                    else:
                        published_dt = datetime.now()
                    
                    # センチメント分析（簡易版）
                    sentiment, sentiment_score = self._analyze_sentiment(
                        entry.get('title', '') + ' ' + entry.get('summary', '')
                    )
                    
                    # キーワード抽出
                    keywords = self._extract_keywords(entry.get('title', ''))
                    
                    articles.append({
                        "title": entry.get('title'),
                        "summary": entry.get('summary'),
                        "url": entry.get('link'),
                        "source": source,
                        "author": entry.get('author'),
                        "published_at": published_dt,
                        "category": self._categorize_article(entry.get('title', '')),
                        "keywords": keywords,
                        "sentiment": sentiment,
                        "sentiment_score": sentiment_score,
                        "views": 0,
                        "likes": 0,
                        "shares": 0
                    })
                    
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error fetching RSS from {source}: {e}")
                continue
        
        return articles
    
    async def _fetch_from_twitter(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Twitterから金融ニュースを取得"""
        articles = []
        
        # フォローする金融ニュースアカウント
        financial_accounts = [
            "Bloomberg", "Reuters", "FT", "WSJ", "CNBC",
            "federalreserve", "BankofEngland", "ecb"
        ]
        
        try:
            # 最近のツイートを取得
            query = " OR ".join([f"from:{account}" for account in financial_accounts])
            
            tweets = self.twitter_client.search_recent_tweets(
                query=query,
                max_results=100,
                tweet_fields=['created_at', 'public_metrics', 'entities'],
                expansions=['author_id'],
                user_fields=['username']
            )
            
            if not tweets.data:
                return articles
            
            for tweet in tweets.data:
                # URL抽出
                urls = []
                if tweet.entities and 'urls' in tweet.entities:
                    urls = [url['expanded_url'] for url in tweet.entities['urls']]
                
                # センチメント分析
                sentiment, sentiment_score = self._analyze_sentiment(tweet.text)
                
                # キーワード抽出
                keywords = self._extract_keywords(tweet.text)
                
                articles.append({
                    "title": tweet.text[:200],  # 最初の200文字
                    "summary": None,
                    "url": urls[0] if urls else f"https://twitter.com/i/web/status/{tweet.id}",
                    "source": "twitter",
                    "author": tweet.author_id,
                    "published_at": tweet.created_at,
                    "category": self._categorize_article(tweet.text),
                    "keywords": keywords,
                    "sentiment": sentiment,
                    "sentiment_score": sentiment_score,
                    "views": tweet.public_metrics['impression_count'],
                    "likes": tweet.public_metrics['like_count'],
                    "shares": tweet.public_metrics['retweet_count']
                })
                
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error fetching Twitter data: {e}")
        
        return articles
    
    def _analyze_sentiment(self, text: str) -> tuple[str, float]:
        """
        センチメント分析（簡易版）
        実際にはOpenAI APIやHugging Face Transformersを使用
        """
        # ポジティブ・ネガティブキーワード
        positive_words = ['gain', 'surge', 'rise', 'bull', 'growth', 'strong', 'beat', 'rally']
        negative_words = ['loss', 'fall', 'drop', 'bear', 'decline', 'weak', 'miss', 'crash']
        
        text_lower = text.lower()
        
        positive_count = sum(1 for word in positive_words if word in text_lower)
        negative_count = sum(1 for word in negative_words if word in text_lower)
        
        if positive_count > negative_count:
            score = min(positive_count / 10, 1.0)
            return "positive", score
        elif negative_count > positive_count:
            score = -min(negative_count / 10, 1.0)
            return "negative", score
        else:
            return "neutral", 0.0
    
    def _extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        """キーワード抽出（簡易版）"""
        keywords = []
        
        # 経済・金融キーワード
        economic_keywords = [
            'inflation', 'GDP', 'unemployment', 'interest rate', 'fed', 'ecb', 'boj',
            'CPI', 'PPI', 'employment', 'retail sales', 'manufacturing', 'housing'
        ]
        
        text_lower = text.lower()
        for keyword in economic_keywords:
            if keyword in text_lower:
                keywords.append(keyword)
        
        return keywords
    
    def _categorize_article(self, text: str) -> str:
        """記事をカテゴリー分類"""
        text_lower = text.lower()
        
        if any(word in text_lower for word in ['gdp', 'inflation', 'cpi', 'employment']):
            return "economy"
        elif any(word in text_lower for word in ['stock', 'market', 'trading', 'dow', 'nasdaq']):
            return "markets"
        elif any(word in text_lower for word in ['fed', 'rate', 'monetary', 'policy', 'central bank']):
            return "policy"
        elif any(word in text_lower for word in ['earnings', 'revenue', 'profit', 'company']):
            return "corporate"
        else:
            return "general"
    
    async def _save_articles_to_db(self, articles: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> int:
        """記事をデータベースに保存"""
        # 実装: SQLAlchemyでのINSERT処理
        # 重複チェック（url）
        saved_count = 0
        
        async with get_db() as db:
            for article in articles:
                # UPSERT処理
                # 実装省略
                saved_count += 1
        
        return saved_count
    
    async def close(self):
        """HTTPクライアントをクローズ"""
        await self.client.aclose()

# グローバルインスタンス
news_service = NewsService()
