"""
AI分析API
OpenAI GPT-4またはAnthropic Claudeを使用した市場データパターン分析
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query, Body
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy import select, and_

from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.ai_analysis import AIAnalysis
from backend.services.features.ai_analysis_service import ai_analysis_service
from backend.core.cache import cache_response

router = APIRouter(tags=["Features - AI Analysis"])

# レスポンススキーマ
class AnalysisRequest(BaseModel):
    data_type: str  # economic_indicator, stock, forex, commodity
    identifier: str  # series_id, symbol, pair, etc.
    time_range_days: int = 90
    analysis_type: str = "comprehensive"  # comprehensive, trend, forecast, anomaly

class AIAnalysisResult(BaseModel):
    id: int
    data_type: str
    identifier: str
    analysis_type: str
    
    # 分析結果
    summary: str
    detailed_analysis: str
    key_insights: List[str]
    
    # トレンド分析
    trend_direction: Optional[str] = None  # upward, downward, sideways
    trend_strength: Optional[float] = None  # 0-1
    
    # パターン検出
    detected_patterns: Optional[List[str]] = None
    
    # 予測
    forecast_direction: Optional[str] = None
    forecast_confidence: Optional[float] = None
    
    # 異常検出
    anomalies_detected: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
    
    # メタデータ
    model_used: str
    confidence_score: float
    generated_at: datetime
    data_period: Dict[str, str]

class AnalysisListResponse(BaseModel):
    analyses: List[AIAnalysisResult]
    total_count: int

@router.post("/generate", response_model=AIAnalysisResult)
async def generate_ai_analysis(request: AnalysisRequest):
    """
    AI分析を生成
    
    data_type:
    - economic_indicator: 経済指標
    - stock: 株式
    - forex: 為替
    - commodity: 商品
    
    analysis_type:
    - comprehensive: 包括的分析
    - trend: トレンド分析
    - forecast: 予測分析
    - anomaly: 異常検出
    """
    try:
        result = await ai_analysis_service.generate_analysis(
            data_type=request.data_type,
            identifier=request.identifier,
            time_range_days=request.time_range_days,
            analysis_type=request.analysis_type
        )
        
        if not result:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=404, 
                detail=f"Unable to generate analysis for {request.identifier}"
            )
        
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error generating AI analysis: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/latest/{data_type}/{identifier}", response_model=AIAnalysisResult)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_latest_analysis(
    data_type: str,
    identifier: str
):
    """
    特定データの最新AI分析を取得
    """
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(AIAnalysis).where(
                and_(
                    AIAnalysis.data_type == data_type,
                    AIAnalysis.identifier == identifier
                )
            ).order_by(AIAnalysis.generated_at.desc()).limit(1)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            analysis = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            
            if not analysis:
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=404,
                    detail=f"No analysis found for {data_type}/{identifier}"
                )
            
            return analysis
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching analysis: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/history/{data_type}/{identifier}", response_model=AnalysisListResponse)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_analysis_history(
    data_type: str,
    identifier: str,
    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=50)
):
    """
    特定データの過去のAI分析履歴を取得
    """
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(AIAnalysis).where(
                and_(
                    AIAnalysis.data_type == data_type,
                    AIAnalysis.identifier == identifier
                )
            ).order_by(AIAnalysis.generated_at.desc()).limit(limit)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            analyses = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "analyses": analyses,
                "total_count": len(analyses)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching analysis history: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/trending-insights", response_model=AnalysisListResponse)
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_trending_insights(
    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=168),
    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=50)
):
    """
    最近のトレンド分析（高信頼度のもの）を取得
    """
    start_time = datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=hours)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(AIAnalysis).where(
                and_(
                    AIAnalysis.generated_at >= start_time,
                    AIAnalysis.confidence_score >= 0.7
                )
            ).order_by(AIAnalysis.confidence_score.desc()).limit(limit)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            analyses = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "analyses": analyses,
                "total_count": len(analyses)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching trending insights: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/anomalies", response_model=AnalysisListResponse)
@cache_response(expire=1800)
async def get_recent_anomalies(
    days: int = Query(7, ge=1, le=30),
    data_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=50)
):
    """
    最近検出された異常パターンを取得
    """
    start_time = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=days)
    
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(AIAnalysis).where(
                and_(
                    AIAnalysis.generated_at >= start_time,
                    AIAnalysis.anomalies_detected.isnot(None),
                    AIAnalysis.anomalies_detected != []
                )
            )
            
            if data_type:
                query = query.where(AIAnalysis.data_type == data_type)
            
            query = query.order_by(AIAnalysis.generated_at.desc()).limit(limit)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            analyses = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "analyses": analyses,
                "total_count": len(analyses)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching anomalies: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/compare")
async def compare_multiple_assets(
    identifiers: List[str] = Body(..., description="List of identifiers to compare"),
    data_type: str = Body(..., description="Data type (stock, forex, commodity)"),
    time_range_days: int = Body(90, ge=7, le=365)
):
    """
    複数の資産を比較分析
    """
    try:
        result = await ai_analysis_service.compare_assets(
            data_type=data_type,
            identifiers=identifiers,
            time_range_days=time_range_days
        )
        
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error comparing assets: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/correlation-analysis")
async def analyze_correlation(
    identifier1: str = Body(...),
    identifier2: str = Body(...),
    data_type1: str = Body(...),
    data_type2: str = Body(...),
    time_range_days: int = Body(90, ge=30, le=365)
):
    """
    2つのデータセット間の相関分析
    例: 米国CPI vs USD/JPY
    """
    try:
        result = await ai_analysis_service.analyze_correlation(
            identifier1=identifier1,
            identifier2=identifier2,
            data_type1=data_type1,
            data_type2=data_type2,
            time_range_days=time_range_days
        )
        
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error analyzing correlation: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/market-summary")
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_market_summary():
    """
    市場全体のAIサマリーを取得
    主要指標の総合分析
    """
    try:
        result = await ai_analysis_service.generate_market_summary()
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error generating market summary: {str(e)}")

"""
AI分析サービス
OpenAI GPT-4またはAnthropic Claudeを使用
"""
import openai
from anthropic import Anthropic
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from scipy import stats
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client
from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.ai_analysis import AIAnalysis
from backend.models.timeseries import EconomicData, MarketData
from backend.config import settings

class AIAnalysisService:
    """AI分析サービス"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        # OpenAI設定
        if settings.OPENAI_API_KEY:
            openai.api_key = settings.OPENAI_API_KEY
            self.use_openai = True
        else:
            self.use_openai = False
        
        # Anthropic設定
        if settings.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
            self.anthropic = Anthropic(api_key=settings.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)
            self.use_anthropic = True
        else:
            self.use_anthropic = False
    
    async def generate_analysis(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifier: str,
        time_range_days: int = 90,
        analysis_type: str = "comprehensive"
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        AI分析を生成
        """
        # キャッシュチェック
        cache_key = f"ai_analysis:{data_type}:{identifier}:{time_range_days}:{analysis_type}"
        if cached := await redis_client.get(cache_key):
            return cached
        
        # データ取得
        data = await self._fetch_data(data_type, identifier, time_range_days)
        
        if not data or len(data) < 10:
            return None
        
        # 統計分析
        stats_results = self._calculate_statistics(data)
        
        # パターン検出
        patterns = self._detect_patterns(data)
        
        # トレンド分析
        trend_info = self._analyze_trend(data)
        
        # 異常検出
        anomalies = self._detect_anomalies(data)
        
        # AIモデルでテキスト生成
        if analysis_type == "comprehensive":
            ai_text = await self._generate_comprehensive_analysis(
                data_type, identifier, data, stats_results, patterns, trend_info, anomalies
            )
        elif analysis_type == "trend":
            ai_text = await self._generate_trend_analysis(
                data_type, identifier, data, trend_info
            )
        elif analysis_type == "forecast":
            ai_text = await self._generate_forecast_analysis(
                data_type, identifier, data, trend_info
            )
        elif analysis_type == "anomaly":
            ai_text = await self._generate_anomaly_analysis(
                data_type, identifier, data, anomalies
            )
        else:
            ai_text = {"summary": "Unknown analysis type", "detailed_analysis": ""}
        
        # 結果を構築
        result = {
            "data_type": data_type,
            "identifier": identifier,
            "analysis_type": analysis_type,
            "summary": ai_text["summary"],
            "detailed_analysis": ai_text["detailed_analysis"],
            "key_insights": ai_text.get("key_insights", []),
            "trend_direction": trend_info["direction"],
            "trend_strength": trend_info["strength"],
            "detected_patterns": patterns,
            "forecast_direction": trend_info.get("forecast_direction"),
            "forecast_confidence": trend_info.get("forecast_confidence"),
            "anomalies_detected": anomalies,
            "model_used": "gpt-4" if self.use_openai else "claude-3-opus",
            "confidence_score": self._calculate_confidence_score(stats_results, data),
            "generated_at": datetime.now(),
            "data_period": {
                "start": data[0]["date"],
                "end": data[-1]["date"]
            }
        }
        
        # データベースに保存
        await self._save_analysis_to_db(result)
        
        # キャッシュ保存（1時間）
        await redis_client.set(cache_key, result, expire=3600)
        
        return result
    
    async def _fetch_data(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifier: str,
        time_range_days: int
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """データを取得"""
        start_date = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=time_range_days)
        
        async with get_db() as db:
            if data_type == "economic_indicator":
                query = select(EconomicData).where(
                    EconomicData.series_id == identifier,
                    EconomicData.date >= start_date
                ).order_by(EconomicData.date)
                
                result = await db.execute(query)
                rows = result.scalars().all()
                
                return [{"date": row.date.isoformat(), "value": row.value} for row in rows]
            
            elif data_type in ["stock", "forex", "commodity"]:
                query = select(MarketData).where(
                    MarketData.symbol == identifier,
                    MarketData.timestamp >= start_date
                ).order_by(MarketData.timestamp)
                
                result = await db.execute(query)
                rows = result.scalars().all()
                
                return [{"date": row.timestamp.isoformat(), "value": row.close} for row in rows]
        
        return []
    
    def _calculate_statistics(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """統計計算"""
        values = [d["value"] for d in data]
        
        return {
            "mean": np.mean(values),
            "median": np.median(values),
            "std": np.std(values),
            "min": np.min(values),
            "max": np.max(values),
            "range": np.max(values) - np.min(values),
            "cv": np.std(values) / np.mean(values) if np.mean(values) != 0 else 0,  # 変動係数
            "skewness": stats.skew(values),
            "kurtosis": stats.kurtosis(values)
        }
    
    def _detect_patterns(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[str]:
        """パターン検出"""
        patterns = []
        values = [d["value"] for d in data]
        
        # トレンド検出
        if len(values) >= 20:
            # 移動平均クロス
            sma_20 = np.convolve(values, np.ones(20)/20, mode='valid')
            sma_50 = np.convolve(values, np.ones(min(50, len(values)))/ min(50, len(values)), mode='valid')
            
            if len(sma_20) > 0 and len(sma_50) > 0:
                if sma_20[-1] > sma_50[-1]:
                    patterns.append("golden_cross")
                elif sma_20[-1] < sma_50[-1]:
                    patterns.append("death_cross")
        
        # ボリンジャーバンド突破
        if len(values) >= 20:
            sma = np.mean(values[-20:])
            std = np.std(values[-20:])
            upper_band = sma + 2 * std
            lower_band = sma - 2 * std
            
            if values[-1] > upper_band:
                patterns.append("overbought")
            elif values[-1] < lower_band:
                patterns.append("oversold")
        
        # 連続上昇/下降
        consecutive_up = 0
        consecutive_down = 0
        for i in range(1, len(values)):
            if values[i] > values[i-1]:
                consecutive_up += 1
                consecutive_down = 0
            elif values[i] < values[i-1]:
                consecutive_down += 1
                consecutive_up = 0
            
            if consecutive_up >= 5:
                patterns.append("strong_uptrend")
                break
            elif consecutive_down >= 5:
                patterns.append("strong_downtrend")
                break
        
        return patterns
    
    def _analyze_trend(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """トレンド分析"""
        values = [d["value"] for d in data]
        
        # 線形回帰
        x = np.arange(len(values))
        slope, intercept, r_value, p_value, std_err = stats.linregress(x, values)
        
        # トレンド方向
        if slope > 0.01:
            direction = "upward"
        elif slope < -0.01:
            direction = "downward"
        else:
            direction = "sideways"
        
        # トレンド強度（R²値を使用）
        strength = r_value ** 2
        
        # 予測
        forecast_value = slope * len(values) + intercept
        forecast_direction = "up" if forecast_value > values[-1] else "down"
        forecast_confidence = strength  # R²値を信頼度として使用
        
        return {
            "direction": direction,
            "strength": float(strength),
            "slope": float(slope),
            "r_squared": float(r_value ** 2),
            "forecast_direction": forecast_direction,
            "forecast_confidence": float(forecast_confidence)
        }
    
    def _detect_anomalies(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """異常検出（Z-scoreベース）"""
        values = [d["value"] for d in data]
        mean = np.mean(values)
        std = np.std(values)
        
        anomalies = []
        for i, d in enumerate(data):
            z_score = (d["value"] - mean) / std if std != 0 else 0
            
            if abs(z_score) > 2.5:  # 2.5σ以上を異常とする
                anomalies.append({
                    "date": d["date"],
                    "value": d["value"],
                    "z_score": float(z_score),
                    "severity": "high" if abs(z_score) > 3 else "medium"
                })
        
        return anomalies
    
    async def _generate_comprehensive_analysis(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifier: str,
        data: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        stats: Dict[str, float],
        patterns: List[str],
        trend: Dict[str, Any],
        anomalies: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """包括的分析テキストをAIで生成"""
        
        prompt = f"""
You are a financial analyst. Analyze the following {data_type} data for {identifier}:

Data Summary:
- Period: {data[0]['date']} to {data[-1]['date']}
- Data points: {len(data)}
- Mean: {stats['mean']:.2f}
- Std Dev: {stats['std']:.2f}
- Range: {stats['min']:.2f} to {stats['max']:.2f}

Trend Analysis:
- Direction: {trend['direction']}
- Strength: {trend['strength']:.2f}
- R-squared: {trend['r_squared']:.2f}

Detected Patterns: {', '.join(patterns) if patterns else 'None'}

Anomalies Detected: {len(anomalies)} significant anomalies

Please provide:
1. A brief summary (2-3 sentences)
2. Detailed analysis (5-7 sentences covering trend, patterns, and implications)
3. 3-5 key insights as bullet points

Format your response as JSON with keys: summary, detailed_analysis, key_insights (array)
"""
        
        if self.use_openai:
            response = await self._call_openai(prompt)
        elif self.use_anthropic:
            response = await self._call_anthropic(prompt)
        else:
            # フォールバック
            response = {
                "summary": f"Analysis of {identifier} shows {trend['direction']} trend with {trend['strength']:.2f} strength.",
                "detailed_analysis": f"The data exhibits a {trend['direction']} trend over the period. Detected patterns include: {', '.join(patterns)}. Statistical analysis reveals mean value of {stats['mean']:.2f} with standard deviation of {stats['std']:.2f}.",
                "key_insights": [
                    f"Trend direction: {trend['direction']}",
                    f"Trend strength: {trend['strength']:.2f}",
                    f"Anomalies detected: {len(anomalies)}"
                ]
            }
        
        return response
    
    async def _generate_trend_analysis(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifier: str,
        data: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        trend: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """トレンド分析テキストを生成"""
        
        prompt = f"""
Analyze the trend for {data_type} {identifier}:

Trend Details:
- Direction: {trend['direction']}
- Strength: {trend['strength']:.2f}
- Slope: {trend['slope']:.4f}

Provide a concise trend analysis focusing on:
1. Current trend direction and strength
2. Momentum assessment
3. Potential continuation or reversal signals

Format as JSON with keys: summary, detailed_analysis, key_insights
"""
        
        if self.use_openai:
            response = await self._call_openai(prompt)
        elif self.use_anthropic:
            response = await self._call_anthropic(prompt)
        else:
            response = {
                "summary": f"{identifier} shows {trend['direction']} trend.",
                "detailed_analysis": f"Trend analysis indicates {trend['direction']} movement with strength of {trend['strength']:.2f}.",
                "key_insights": [f"Direction: {trend['direction']}", f"Strength: {trend['strength']:.2f}"]
            }
        
        return response
    
    async def _generate_forecast_analysis(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifier: str,
        data: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        trend: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """予測分析テキストを生成"""
        
        prompt = f"""
Generate a forecast analysis for {data_type} {identifier}:

Current Trend:
- Direction: {trend['direction']}
- Forecast Direction: {trend.get('forecast_direction', 'unknown')}
- Confidence: {trend.get('forecast_confidence', 0):.2f}

Provide:
1. Short-term forecast (next 1-2 periods)
2. Confidence assessment
3. Key risk factors

Format as JSON with keys: summary, detailed_analysis, key_insights
"""
        
        if self.use_openai:
            response = await self._call_openai(prompt)
        elif self.use_anthropic:
            response = await self._call_anthropic(prompt)
        else:
            response = {
                "summary": f"Forecast for {identifier} suggests {trend.get('forecast_direction', 'stable')} movement.",
                "detailed_analysis": f"Based on current trend analysis, expect {trend.get('forecast_direction', 'stable')} direction with {trend.get('forecast_confidence', 0):.2f} confidence.",
                "key_insights": [f"Forecast: {trend.get('forecast_direction', 'stable')}", f"Confidence: {trend.get('forecast_confidence', 0):.2f}"]
            }
        
        return response
    
    async def _generate_anomaly_analysis(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifier: str,
        data: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        anomalies: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """異常分析テキストを生成"""
        
        prompt = f"""
Analyze anomalies detected in {data_type} {identifier}:

Anomalies Found: {len(anomalies)}
Most Recent Anomalies:
{chr(10).join([f"- {a['date']}: value={a['value']:.2f}, z-score={a['z_score']:.2f}" for a in anomalies[:5]])}

Provide:
1. Assessment of anomaly significance
2. Possible causes or explanations
3. Implications for future trends

Format as JSON with keys: summary, detailed_analysis, key_insights
"""
        
        if self.use_openai:
            response = await self._call_openai(prompt)
        elif self.use_anthropic:
            response = await self._call_anthropic(prompt)
        else:
            response = {
                "summary": f"Detected {len(anomalies)} anomalies in {identifier}.",
                "detailed_analysis": f"Analysis found {len(anomalies)} significant deviations from normal patterns.",
                "key_insights": [f"Total anomalies: {len(anomalies)}", "Requires further investigation"]
            }
        
        return response
    
    async def _call_openai(self, prompt: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """OpenAI APIを呼び出し"""
        try:
            response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
                model="gpt-4",
                messages=[
                    {"role": "system", "content": "You are a financial analyst. Respond only with valid JSON."},
                    {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
                ],
                temperature=0.7,
                max_tokens=1000
            )
            
            import json
            return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"OpenAI API error: {e}")
            return {
                "summary": "Error generating analysis",
                "detailed_analysis": str(e),
                "key_insights": []
            }
    
    async def _call_anthropic(self, prompt: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Anthropic Claude APIを呼び出し"""
        try:
            message = self.anthropic.messages.create(
                model="claude-3-opus-20240229",
                max_tokens=1000,
                messages=[
                    {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
                ]
            )
            
            import json
            return json.loads(message.content[0].text)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Anthropic API error: {e}")
            return {
                "summary": "Error generating analysis",
                "detailed_analysis": str(e),
                "key_insights": []
            }
    
    def _calculate_confidence_score(
        self,
        stats: Dict[str, float],
        data: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> float:
        """信頼度スコアを計算"""
        # データポイント数
        data_score = min(len(data) / 100, 1.0)
        
        # 変動係数（低いほど安定）
        cv_score = 1.0 - min(stats['cv'], 1.0)
        
        # 総合スコア
        confidence = (data_score * 0.4 + cv_score * 0.6)
        
        return round(confidence, 3)
    
    async def _save_analysis_to_db(self, analysis: Dict[str, Any]):
        """分析結果をデータベースに保存"""
        # 実装: SQLAlchemyでのINSERT処理
        async with get_db() as db:
            # 実装省略
            pass
    
    async def compare_assets(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifiers: List[str],
        time_range_days: int
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """複数資産の比較分析"""
        
        all_data = {}
        for identifier in identifiers:
            data = await self._fetch_data(data_type, identifier, time_range_days)
            if data:
                all_data[identifier] = data
        
        # 相関行列計算
        correlation_matrix = self._calculate_correlation_matrix(all_data)
        
        # 各資産の統計
        asset_stats = {}
        for identifier, data in all_data.items():
            asset_stats[identifier] = self._calculate_statistics(data)
        
        return {
            "assets": identifiers,
            "correlation_matrix": correlation_matrix,
            "asset_statistics": asset_stats,
            "comparison_summary": f"Comparing {len(identifiers)} {data_type} assets over {time_range_days} days"
        }
    
    def _calculate_correlation_matrix(
        self,
        all_data: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]
    ) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]:
        """相関行列を計算"""
        identifiers = list(all_data.keys())
        matrix = {}
        
        for id1 in identifiers:
            matrix[id1] = {}
            values1 = [d["value"] for d in all_data[id1]]
            
            for id2 in identifiers:
                values2 = [d["value"] for d in all_data[id2]]
                
                # 長さを揃える
                min_len = min(len(values1), len(values2))
                correlation = np.corrcoef(values1[:min_len], values2[:min_len])[0, 1]
                
                matrix[id1][id2] = float(correlation)
        
        return matrix
    
    async def analyze_correlation(
        self,
        identifier1: str,
        identifier2: str,
        data_type1: str,
        data_type2: str,
        time_range_days: int
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """2つのデータセット間の相関分析"""
        
        data1 = await self._fetch_data(data_type1, identifier1, time_range_days)
        data2 = await self._fetch_data(data_type2, identifier2, time_range_days)
        
        if not data1 or not data2:
            return {"error": "Insufficient data"}
        
        # 相関係数計算
        values1 = [d["value"] for d in data1]
        values2 = [d["value"] for d in data2]
        
        min_len = min(len(values1), len(values2))
        correlation = np.corrcoef(values1[:min_len], values2[:min_len])[0, 1]
        
        # 解釈
        if abs(correlation) > 0.7:
            strength = "strong"
        elif abs(correlation) > 0.4:
            strength = "moderate"
        else:
            strength = "weak"
        
        direction = "positive" if correlation > 0 else "negative"
        
        return {
            "identifier1": identifier1,
            "identifier2": identifier2,
            "correlation_coefficient": float(correlation),
            "correlation_strength": strength,
            "correlation_direction": direction,
            "interpretation": f"{strength.capitalize()} {direction} correlation between {identifier1} and {identifier2}"
        }
    
    async def generate_market_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """市場全体のサマリーを生成"""
        
        # 主要指標のリスト
        key_indicators = [
            {"type": "stock", "id": "^GSPC", "name": "S&P 500"},
            {"type": "stock", "id": "^DJI", "name": "Dow Jones"},
            {"type": "stock", "id": "^IXIC", "name": "NASDAQ"},
            {"type": "forex", "id": "USD/JPY", "name": "USD/JPY"},
            {"type": "commodity", "id": "gold", "name": "Gold"}
        ]
        
        summaries = []
        for indicator in key_indicators:
            try:
                analysis = await self.generate_analysis(
                    data_type=indicator["type"],
                    identifier=indicator["id"],
                    time_range_days=30,
                    analysis_type="trend"
                )
                
                if analysis:
                    summaries.append({
                        "name": indicator["name"],
                        "trend": analysis["trend_direction"],
                        "strength": analysis["trend_strength"]
                    })
            except:
                continue
        
        return {
            "summary_date": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "indicators": summaries,
            "overall_sentiment": "mixed"  # 実際にはセンチメント集計
        }

backend/routers/features/notifications.py
"""
通知システムAPI
Discord、LINE通知の設定と送信
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query, Body
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy import select, and_

from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.notifications import NotificationSubscription, NotificationLog
from backend.services.features.notification_service import notification_service
from backend.core.cache import cache_response

router = APIRouter(tags=["Features - Notifications"])

# レスポンススキーマ
class NotificationSubscriptionRequest(BaseModel):
    user_id: str
    platform: str  # discord, line
    channel_id: str  # Discord channel ID or LINE user ID
    notification_types: List[str]  # economic_data, earnings, news, ai_insights
    filters: Optional[dict] = None  # 通知フィルター条件

class SubscriptionResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    user_id: str
    platform: str
    channel_id: str
    notification_types: List[str]
    filters: Optional[dict]
    is_active: bool
    created_at: datetime

class NotificationTestRequest(BaseModel):
    platform: str
    channel_id: str
    message: str

@router.post("/subscribe", response_model=SubscriptionResponse)
async def create_subscription(request: NotificationSubscriptionRequest):
    """
    通知サブスクリプションを作成
    
    platform: discord, line
    notification_types:
    - economic_data: 経済指標発表
    - earnings: 決算発表
    - news: 重要ニュース
    - ai_insights: AI分析結果
    - price_alerts: 価格アラート
    """
    try:
        subscription = await notification_service.create_subscription(
            user_id=request.user_id,
            platform=request.platform,
            channel_id=request.channel_id,
            notification_types=request.notification_types,
            filters=request.filters
        )
        
        return subscription
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error creating subscription: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/subscriptions/{user_id}", response_model=List[SubscriptionResponse])
async def get_user_subscriptions(user_id: str):
    """ユーザーの全サブスクリプションを取得"""
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(NotificationSubscription).where(
                NotificationSubscription.user_id == user_id
            )
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            subscriptions = result.scalars().all()
            
            return subscriptions
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching subscriptions: {str(e)}")

@router.put("/subscriptions/{subscription_id}")
async def update_subscription(
    subscription_id: int,
    notification_types: Optional[List[str]] = Body(None),
    filters: Optional[dict] = Body(None),
    is_active: Optional[bool] = Body(None)
):
    """サブスクリプションを更新"""
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(NotificationSubscription).where(
                NotificationSubscription.id == subscription_id
            )
            result = await db.execute(query)
            subscription = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            
            if not subscription:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Subscription not found")
            
            if notification_types is not None:
                subscription.notification_types = notification_types
            if filters is not None:
                subscription.filters = filters
            if is_active is not None:
                subscription.is_active = is_active
            
            subscription.updated_at = datetime.now()
            await db.commit()
            
            return {"status": "success", "subscription_id": subscription_id}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error updating subscription: {str(e)}")

@router.delete("/subscriptions/{subscription_id}")
async def delete_subscription(subscription_id: int):
    """サブスクリプションを削除"""
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(NotificationSubscription).where(
                NotificationSubscription.id == subscription_id
            )
            result = await db.execute(query)
            subscription = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            
            if not subscription:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Subscription not found")
            
            await db.delete(subscription)
            await db.commit()
            
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Subscription deleted"}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error deleting subscription: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/test")
async def test_notification(request: NotificationTestRequest):
    """
    通知テスト送信
    接続確認用
    """
    try:
        if request.platform == "discord":
            result = await notification_service.send_discord_message(
                channel_id=request.channel_id,
                message=request.message
            )
        elif request.platform == "line":
            result = await notification_service.send_line_message(
                user_id=request.channel_id,
                message=request.message
            )
        else:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid platform")
        
        return {
            "status": "success" if result else "failed",
            "platform": request.platform,
            "message": "Test notification sent"
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error sending test notification: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/send/economic-alert")
async def send_economic_alert(
    indicator_name: str = Body(...),
    country: str = Body(...),
    actual: float = Body(...),
    forecast: Optional[float] = Body(None),
    previous: Optional[float] = Body(None)
):
    """
    経済指標アラートを送信
    （内部API、Celeryタスクから呼ばれる）
    """
    try:
        await notification_service.send_economic_data_alert(
            indicator_name=indicator_name,
            country=country,
            actual=actual,
            forecast=forecast,
            previous=previous
        )
        
        return {"status": "success", "message": "Economic alert sent"}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error sending alert: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/send/earnings-alert")
async def send_earnings_alert(
    symbol: str = Body(...),
    company_name: str = Body(...),
    eps_actual: float = Body(...),
    eps_estimate: Optional[float] = Body(None),
    surprise_percent: Optional[float] = Body(None)
):
    """
    決算アラートを送信
    """
    try:
        await notification_service.send_earnings_alert(
            symbol=symbol,
            company_name=company_name,
            eps_actual=eps_actual,
            eps_estimate=eps_estimate,
            surprise_percent=surprise_percent
        )
        
        return {"status": "success", "message": "Earnings alert sent"}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error sending alert: {str(e)}")

@router.post("/send/price-alert")
async def send_price_alert(
    symbol: str = Body(...),
    asset_type: str = Body(...),
    current_price: float = Body(...),
    trigger_price: float = Body(...),
    direction: str = Body(...)  # above, below
):
    """
    価格アラートを送信
    """
    try:
        await notification_service.send_price_alert(
            symbol=symbol,
            asset_type=asset_type,
            current_price=current_price,
            trigger_price=trigger_price,
            direction=direction
        )
        
        return {"status": "success", "message": "Price alert sent"}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error sending alert: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/logs/{user_id}")
async def get_notification_logs(
    user_id: str,
    limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200)
):
    """ユーザーの通知履歴を取得"""
    try:
        async with get_db() as db:
            query = select(NotificationLog).where(
                NotificationLog.user_id == user_id
            ).order_by(NotificationLog.sent_at.desc()).limit(limit)
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            logs = result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "logs": logs,
                "total_count": len(logs)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error fetching logs: {str(e)}")

backend/services/features/notification_service.py
"""
通知サービス
Discord Webhook、LINE Messaging APIを使用
"""
import httpx
import json
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client
from backend.core.database import get_db
from backend.models.notifications import NotificationSubscription, NotificationLog
from backend.config import settings

class NotificationService:
    """通知サービス"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0)
        self.discord_webhooks = {}  # チャンネルID -> Webhook URL
        self.line_access_token = settings.LINE_CHANNEL_ACCESS_TOKEN
    
    async def create_subscription(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        platform: str,
        channel_id: str,
        notification_types: List[str],
        filters: Optional[dict] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """サブスクリプションを作成"""
        
        async with get_db() as db:
            subscription = NotificationSubscription(
                user_id=user_id,
                platform=platform,
                channel_id=channel_id,
                notification_types=notification_types,
                filters=filters or {},
                is_active=True,
                created_at=datetime.now()
            )
            
            db.add(subscription)
            await db.commit()
            await db.refresh(subscription)
            
            return {
                "id": subscription.id,
                "user_id": subscription.user_id,
                "platform": subscription.platform,
                "channel_id": subscription.channel_id,
                "notification_types": subscription.notification_types,
                "filters": subscription.filters,
                "is_active": subscription.is_active,
                "created_at": subscription.created_at
            }
    
    async def send_discord_message(
        self,
        channel_id: str,
        message: str,
        embed: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    ) -> bool:
        """Discordメッセージを送信"""
        
        # Webhook URLを取得（設定から）
        webhook_url = self.discord_webhooks.get(channel_id) or settings.DISCORD_WEBHOOK_URL
        
        if not webhook_url:
            print(f"Discord webhook URL not found for channel {channel_id}")
            return False
        
        try:
            payload = {"content": message}
            
            if embed:
                payload["embeds"] = [embed]
            
            response = await self.client.post(webhook_url, json=payload)
            response.raise_for_status()
            
            return True
        except httpx.HTTPError as e:
            print(f"Error sending Discord message: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def send_line_message(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        message: str
    ) -> bool:
        """LINEメッセージを送信"""
        
        if not self.line_access_token:
            print("LINE access token not configured")
            return False
        
        url = "https://api.line.me/v2/bot/message/push"
        headers = {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.line_access_token}"
        }
        
        payload = {
            "to": user_id,
            "messages": [
                {
                    "type": "text",
                    "text": message
                }
            ]
        }
        
        try:
            response = await self.client.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload)
            response.raise_for_status()
            
            return True
        except httpx.HTTPError as e:
            print(f"Error sending LINE message: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def send_economic_data_alert(
        self,
        indicator_name: str,
        country: str,
        actual: float,
        forecast: Optional[float] = None,
        previous: Optional[float] = None
    ):
        """経済指標アラートを送信"""
        
        # サプライズ計算
        surprise = ""
        if forecast is not None:
            diff = actual - forecast
            diff_percent = (diff / forecast * 100) if forecast != 0 else 0
            
            if abs(diff_percent) > 10:
                surprise = f" 🚨 大幅サプライズ ({diff_percent:+.1f}%)"
            elif diff > 0:
                surprise = f" ✅ 予想上回る ({diff_percent:+.1f}%)"
            else:
                surprise = f" ⚠️ 予想下回る ({diff_percent:+.1f}%)"
        
        # メッセージ作成
        message = f"""
📊 **経済指標発表: {country} - {indicator_name}**

実績値: **{actual}**{surprise}
予想値: {forecast if forecast is not None else 'N/A'}
前回値: {previous if previous is not None else 'N/A'}

発表日時: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}
"""
        
        # Discord Embed
        embed = {
            "title": f"{country} - {indicator_name}",
            "description": f"実績: **{actual}**{surprise}",
            "color": 0x00ff00 if (forecast and actual > forecast) else 0xff0000,
            "fields": [
                {"name": "予想", "value": str(forecast) if forecast else "N/A", "inline": True},
                {"name": "前回", "value": str(previous) if previous else "N/A", "inline": True}
            ],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # アクティブなサブスクリプションを取得
        await self._send_to_subscribers(
            notification_type="economic_data",
            message=message,
            embed=embed,
            filters={"country": country}
        )
    
    async def send_earnings_alert(
        self,
        symbol: str,
        company_name: str,
        eps_actual: float,
        eps_estimate: Optional[float] = None,
        surprise_percent: Optional[float] = None
    ):
        """決算アラートを送信"""
        
        surprise = ""
        if surprise_percent is not None:
            if surprise_percent > 5:
                surprise = f" ✅ 予想上回る (+{surprise_percent:.1f}%)"
            elif surprise_percent < -5:
                surprise = f" ⚠️ 予想下回る ({surprise_percent:.1f}%)"
            else:
                surprise = " 📊 ほぼ予想通り"
        
        message = f"""
📈 **決算発表: {symbol} - {company_name}**

EPS実績: **${eps_actual:.2f}**{surprise}
EPS予想: ${eps_estimate:.2f if eps_estimate else 'N/A'}

発表日時: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}
"""
        
        embed = {
            "title": f"{symbol} - {company_name} 決算",
            "description": f"EPS: **${eps_actual:.2f}**{surprise}",
            "color": 0x00ff00 if (surprise_percent and surprise_percent > 0) else 0xff0000,
            "fields": [
                {"name": "EPS予想", "value": f"${eps_estimate:.2f}" if eps_estimate else "N/A", "inline": True},
                {"name": "サプライズ", "value": f"{surprise_percent:+.1f}%" if surprise_percent else "N/A", "inline": True}
            ],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        await self._send_to_subscribers(
            notification_type="earnings",
            message=message,
            embed=embed,
            filters={"symbol": symbol}
        )
    
    async def send_price_alert(
        self,
        symbol: str,
        asset_type: str,
        current_price: float,
        trigger_price: float,
        direction: str
    ):
        """価格アラートを送信"""
        
        direction_text = "上回りました" if direction == "above" else "下回りました"
        emoji = "🔥" if direction == "above" else "❄️"
        
        message = f"""
{emoji} **価格アラート: {symbol}**

現在価格: **{current_price}**
トリガー価格: {trigger_price}

{symbol}が{trigger_price}を{direction_text}

時刻: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}
"""
        
        embed = {
            "title": f"価格アラート: {symbol}",
            "description": f"現在価格: **{current_price}**",
            "color": 0xff9900,
            "fields": [
                {"name": "トリガー価格", "value": str(trigger_price), "inline": True},
                {"name": "方向", "value": direction_text, "inline": True}
            ],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        await self._send_to_subscribers(
            notification_type="price_alerts",
            message=message,
            embed=embed,
            filters={"symbol": symbol, "asset_type": asset_type}
        )
    
    async def send_news_alert(
        self,
        title: str,
        summary: str,
        url: str,
        source: str,
        sentiment: Optional[str] = None
    ):
        """ニュースアラートを送信"""
        
        sentiment_emoji = {
            "positive": "📈",
            "negative": "📉",
            "neutral": "📊"
        }.get(sentiment, "📰")
        
        message = f"""
{sentiment_emoji} **ニュース速報**

**{title}**

{summary[:200]}...

ソース: {source}
詳細: {url}
"""
        
        embed = {
            "title": title,
            "description": summary[:500],
            "url": url,
            "color": 0x3498db,
            "footer": {"text": f"Source: {source}"},
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        await self._send_to_subscribers(
            notification_type="news",
            message=message,
            embed=embed
        )
    
    async def send_ai_insight_alert(
        self,
        identifier: str,
        data_type: str,
        summary: str,
        key_insights: List[str],
        confidence: float
    ):
        """AI分析アラートを送信"""
        
        insights_text = "\n".join([f"• {insight}" for insight in key_insights[:3]])
        
        message = f"""
🤖 **AI分析レポート: {identifier}**

{summary}

**主要インサイト:**
{insights_text}

信頼度: {confidence * 100:.0f}%
生成時刻: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}
"""
        
        embed = {
            "title": f"AI分析: {identifier}",
            "description": summary,
            "color": 0x9b59b6,
            "fields": [
                {"name": "データタイプ", "value": data_type, "inline": True},
                {"name": "信頼度", "value": f"{confidence * 100:.0f}%", "inline": True}
            ],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        await self._send_to_subscribers(
            notification_type="ai_insights",
            message=message,
            embed=embed,
            filters={"identifier": identifier}
        )
    
    async def _send_to_subscribers(
        self,
        notification_type: str,
        message: str,
        embed: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        filters: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    ):
        """サブスクライバーに通知を送信"""
        
        async with get_db() as db:
            # アクティブなサブスクリプションを取得
            query = select(NotificationSubscription).where(
                NotificationSubscription.is_active == True,
                NotificationSubscription.notification_types.contains([notification_type])
            )
            
            result = await db.execute(query)
            subscriptions = result.scalars().all()
            
            for sub in subscriptions:
                # フィルター適用
                if filters and sub.filters:
                    if not self._match_filters(filters, sub.filters):
                        continue
                
                # 送信
                success = False
                if sub.platform == "discord":
                    success = await self.send_discord_message(
                        channel_id=sub.channel_id,
                        message=message,
                        embed=embed
                    )
                elif sub.platform == "line":
                    success = await self.send_line_message(
                        user_id=sub.channel_id,
                        message=message
                    )
                
                # ログ保存
                await self._log_notification(
                    user_id=sub.user_id,
                    subscription_id=sub.id,
                    notification_type=notification_type,
                    platform=sub.platform,
                    success=success,
                    message=message[:500]
                )
    
    def _match_filters(self, data: Dict[str, Any], filters: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """フィルター条件にマッチするか確認"""
        for key, value in filters.items():
            if key in data:
                if isinstance(value, list):
                    if data[key] not in value:
                        return False
                else:
                    if data[key] != value:
                        return False
        return True
    
    async def _log_notification(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        subscription_id: int,
        notification_type: str,
        platform: str,
        success: bool,
        message: str
    ):
        """通知ログを保存"""
        async with get_db() as db:
            log = NotificationLog(
                user_id=user_id,
                subscription_id=subscription_id,
                notification_type=notification_type,
                platform=platform,
                success=success,
                message=message,
                sent_at=datetime.now()
            )
            
            db.add(log)
            await db.commit()
    
    async def close(self):
        """HTTPクライアントをクローズ"""
        await self.client.aclose()

# グローバルインスタンス
notification_service = NotificationService()

フロントエンドの主要コンポーネント実装例
frontend/src/lib/api.ts（APIクライアント）
import axios from 'axios';

const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:8000';

export const apiClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
  timeout: 30000,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

// レスポンスインターセプター
apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    console.error('API Error:', error.response?.data || error.message);
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

// API関数
export const economicAPI = {
  // USA FRED
  getFREDSeries: (seriesId: string, startDate?: string, endDate?: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/usa/fred/series/${seriesId}`, { params: { start_date: startDate, end_date: endDate } }),
  
  getUSCPI: () => apiClient.get('/api/usa/fred/cpi'),
  
  // Japan BOJ
  getBOJSeries: (seriesId: string, startDate?: string, endDate?: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/japan/boj/series/${seriesId}`, { params: { start_date: startDate, end_date: endDate } }),
  
  // UK ONS
  getUKCPI: (startDate?: string, endDate?: string) =>
    apiClient.get('/api/uk/ons/cpi', { params: { start_date: startDate, end_date: endDate } }),
};

export const marketAPI = {
  // Stocks
  getStockQuote: (symbol: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/markets/stocks/quote/${symbol}`),
  
  getStockHistory: (symbol: string, interval: string, period: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/markets/stocks/history/${symbol}`, { params: { interval, period } }),
  
  // Forex
  getForexQuote: (base: string, quote: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/markets/forex/quote/${base}/${quote}`),
  
  getForexHistory: (base: string, quote: string, interval: string, period: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/markets/forex/history/${base}/${quote}`, { params: { interval, period } }),
  
  // Commodities
  getCommodityQuote: (commodityId: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/markets/commodities/quote/${commodityId}`),
  
  getAllMetals: () => apiClient.get('/api/markets/commodities/metals/all'),
};

export const featuresAPI = {
  // Calendar
  getCalendarEvents: (params: any) =>
    apiClient.get('/api/features/calendar/events', { params }),
  
  getTodayEvents: () =>
    apiClient.get('/api/features/calendar/today'),
  
  // Earnings
  getEarningsCalendar: (params: any) =>
    apiClient.get('/api/features/earnings/calendar', { params }),
  
  getTodayEarnings: () =>
    apiClient.get('/api/features/earnings/today'),
  
  // News
  getNewsHeadlines: (params: any) =>
    apiClient.get('/api/features/news/headlines', { params }),
  
  getTrendingNews: (hours: number = 6) =>
    apiClient.get('/api/features/news/trending', { params: { hours } }),
  
  // AI Analysis
  generateAnalysis: (data: any) =>
    apiClient.post('/api/features/ai-analysis/generate', data),
  
  getLatestAnalysis: (dataType: string, identifier: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/features/ai-analysis/latest/${dataType}/${identifier}`),
  
  // Notifications
  createSubscription: (data: any) =>
    apiClient.post('/api/features/notifications/subscribe', data),
  
  getUserSubscriptions: (userId: string) =>
    apiClient.get(`/api/features/notifications/subscriptions/${userId}`),
};

frontend/src/components/charts/LineChart.tsx
import React from 'react';
import {
  LineChart as RechartsLineChart,
  Line,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
  ResponsiveContainer,
} from 'recharts';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

interface DataPoint {
  date: string;
  value: number;
  [key: string]: any;
}

interface LineChartProps {
  data: DataPoint[];
  dataKey: string;
  xAxisKey?: string;
  title?: string;
  color?: string;
  height?: number;
  showGrid?: boolean;
  formatYAxis?: (value: number) => string;
  formatTooltip?: (value: number) => string;
}

export const LineChart: React.FC<LineChartProps> = ({
  data,
  dataKey,
  xAxisKey = 'date',
  title,
  color = '#0ea5e9',
  height = 400,
  showGrid = true,
  formatYAxis,
  formatTooltip,
}) => {
  const formatXAxis = (value: string) => {
    try {
      return format(new Date(value), 'MMM yyyy');
    } catch {
      return value;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      {title && (
        <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-4 text-gray-800">{title}</h3>
      )}
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={height}>
        <RechartsLineChart data={data} margin={{ top: 5, right: 30, left: 20, bottom: 5 }}>
          {showGrid && <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />}
          <XAxis
            dataKey={xAxisKey}
            tickFormatter={formatXAxis}
            stroke="#6b7280"
            style={{ fontSize: '12px' }}
          />
          <YAxis
            tickFormatter={formatYAxis}
            stroke="#6b7280"
            style={{ fontSize: '12px' }}
          />
          <Tooltip
            formatter={formatTooltip || ((value: number) => value.toFixed(2))}
            labelFormatter={(label) => format(new Date(label), 'PPP')}
            contentStyle={{
              backgroundColor: 'white',
              border: '1px solid #e5e7eb',
              borderRadius: '8px',
            }}
          />
          <Legend />
          <Line
            type="monotone"
            dataKey={dataKey}
            stroke={color}
            strokeWidth={2}
            dot={false}
            activeDot={{ r: 6 }}
          />
        </RechartsLineChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </div>
  );
};

frontend/src/components/features/EconomicCalendar.tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { featuresAPI } from '@/lib/api';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { CalendarIcon, TrendingUpIcon, TrendingDownIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline';

interface CalendarEvent {
  id: number;
  country: string;
  indicator_name: string;
  release_date: string;
  importance: string;
  forecast: number | null;
  previous: number | null;
  actual: number | null;
  beat_miss: string | null;
}

export const EconomicCalendar: React.FC = () => {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'high'>('all');

  const { data, isLoading, error } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['calendar-events', filter],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const endpoint = filter === 'high' 
        ? featuresAPI.getTodayEvents
        : featuresAPI.getTodayEvents;
      
      const response = await endpoint();
      return response.data;
    },
    refetchInterval: 60000, // 1分ごとに更新
  });

  const getImportanceColor = (importance: string) => {
    switch (importance) {
      case 'high': return 'bg-red-100 text-red-800';
      case 'medium': return 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800';
      case 'low': return 'bg-green-100 text-green-800';
      default: return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800';
    }
  };

  const getBeatMissIcon = (beatMiss: string | null) => {
    if (beatMiss === 'beat') return <TrendingUpIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-green-600" />;
    if (beatMiss === 'miss') return <TrendingDownIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-red-600" />;
    return null;
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-64">
        <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-12 w-12 border-b-2 border-primary-500"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
        <p className="text-red-800">Error loading calendar events</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg p-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <CalendarIcon className="w-6 h-6 text-primary-600" />
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">Economic Calendar</h2>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setFilter('all')}
            className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
              filter === 'all'
                ? 'bg-primary-600 text-white'
                : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            All Events
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setFilter('high')}
            className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
              filter === 'high'
                ? 'bg-primary-600 text-white'
                : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            High Impact Only
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {data?.events?.map((event: CalendarEvent) => (
          <div
            key={event.id}
            className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-4 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow"
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                  <span className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">
                    {event.country}
                  </span>
                  <span className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium ${getImportanceColor(event.importance)}`}>
                    {event.importance.toUpperCase()}
                  </span>
                  {getBeatMissIcon(event.beat_miss)}
                </div>
                
                <h3 className="text-base font-medium text-gray-800 mb-2">
                  {event.indicator_name}
                </h3>
                
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-sm">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-gray-500">Forecast:</span>
                    <span className="ml-2 font-medium text-gray-900">
                      {event.forecast !== null ? event.forecast : 'N/A'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-gray-500">Previous:</span>
                    <span className="ml-2 font-medium text-gray-900">
                      {event.previous !== null ? event.previous : 'N/A'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-gray-500">Actual:</span>
                    <span className={`ml-2 font-bold ${
                      event.actual !== null
                        ? event.beat_miss === 'beat'
                          ? 'text-green-600'
                          : event.beat_miss === 'miss'
                          ? 'text-red-600'
                          : 'text-gray-900'
                        : 'text-gray-400'
                    }`}>
                      {event.actual !== null ? event.actual : 'Pending'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <div className="text-right text-sm text-gray-500">
                {format(new Date(event.release_date), 'HH:mm')}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {(!data?.events || data.events.length === 0) && (
        <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-500">
          <CalendarIcon className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 text-gray-400" />
          <p>No events scheduled for today</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

frontend/src/components/features/MarketOverview.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { marketAPI } from '@/lib/api';
import { ArrowUpIcon, ArrowDownIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/solid';

interface MarketQuote {
  symbol: string;
  name?: string;
  price: number;
  change: number;
  change_percent: number;
}

const MAJOR_INDICES = [
  { symbol: '^GSPC', name: 'S&P 500' },
  { symbol: '^DJI', name: 'Dow Jones' },
  { symbol: '^IXIC', name: 'NASDAQ' },
];

const MAJOR_FOREX = [
  { base: 'USD', quote: 'JPY', name: 'USD/JPY' },
  { base: 'EUR', quote: 'USD', name: 'EUR/USD' },
  { base: 'GBP', quote: 'USD', name: 'GBP/USD' },
];

export const MarketOverview: React.FC = () => {
  // 株式指数
  const indicesQueries = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['market-indices'],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const promises = MAJOR_INDICES.map(index => 
        marketAPI.getStockQuote(index.symbol)
      );
      const results = await Promise.all(promises);
      return results.map((r, i) => ({ ...r.data, name: MAJOR_INDICES[i].name }));
    },
    refetchInterval: 60000,
  });

  // 為替
  const forexQueries = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['major-forex'],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const promises = MAJOR_FOREX.map(pair => 
        marketAPI.getForexQuote(pair.base, pair.quote)
      );
      const results = await Promise.all(promises);
      return results.map((r, i) => ({ ...r.data, name: MAJOR_FOREX[i].name }));
    },
    refetchInterval: 60000,
  });

  // 貴金属
  const metalsQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['major-metals'],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const response = await marketAPI.getAllMetals();
      return response.data;
    },
    refetchInterval: 60000,
  });

  const renderQuoteCard = (quote: MarketQuote) => {
    const isPositive = quote.change >= 0;
    
    return (
      <div key={quote.symbol} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow p-4 hover:shadow-lg transition-shadow">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-2">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">{quote.name || quote.symbol}</h3>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mt-1">
              {quote.price.toFixed(2)}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded ${
            isPositive ? 'bg-green-100' : 'bg-red-100'
          }`}>
            {isPositive ? (
              <ArrowUpIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-green-600" />
            ) : (
              <ArrowDownIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600" />
            )}
            <span className={`text-sm font-semibold ${
              isPositive ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-600'
            }`}>
              {Math.abs(quote.change_percent).toFixed(2)}%
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className={`text-sm ${isPositive ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-600'}`}>
          {isPositive ? '+' : ''}{quote.change.toFixed(2)}
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* 株式指数 */}
      <section>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Major Indices</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {indicesQueries.isLoading ? (
            <div className="col-span-3 flex justify-center py-8">
              <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-b-2 border-primary-500"></div>
            </div>
          ) : indicesQueries.data ? (
            indicesQueries.data.map(renderQuoteCard)
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 為替 */}
      <section>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Major Currencies</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {forexQueries.isLoading ? (
            <div className="col-span-3 flex justify-center py-8">
              <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-b-2 border-primary-500"></div>
            </div>
          ) : forexQueries.data ? (
            forexQueries.data.map(renderQuoteCard)
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 貴金属 */}
      <section>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Precious Metals</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {metalsQuery.isLoading ? (
            <div className="col-span-3 flex justify-center py-8">
              <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-b-2 border-primary-500"></div>
            </div>
          ) : metalsQuery.data ? (
            metalsQuery.data.map(renderQuoteCard)
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
};


4.5 Docker Compose設定（ローカル開発環境）
docker-compose.yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  # PostgreSQL + TimescaleDB
  postgres:
    image: timescale/timescaledb:latest-pg15
    container_name: economic-platform-db
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: economic_platform
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
      PGDATA: /var/lib/postgresql/data/pgdata
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    networks:
      - economic-network
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U postgres"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5

  # Redis
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    container_name: economic-platform-redis
    command: redis-server --appendonly yes
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
    volumes:
      - redis_data:/data
    networks:
      - economic-network
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "redis-cli", "ping"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 5

  # Backend (FastAPI)
  backend:
    build:
      context: ./backend
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: economic-platform-backend
    command: uvicorn main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://postgres:postgres@postgres:5432/economic_platform
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379/0
      FRED_API_KEY: ${FRED_API_KEY}
      ALPHA_VANTAGE_API_KEY: ${ALPHA_VANTAGE_API_KEY}
      OPENAI_API_KEY: ${OPENAI_API_KEY}
      ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${ANTHROPIC_API_KEY}
      TWITTER_BEARER_TOKEN: ${TWITTER_BEARER_TOKEN}
      DISCORD_WEBHOOK_URL: ${DISCORD_WEBHOOK_URL}
      LINE_CHANNEL_ACCESS_TOKEN: ${LINE_CHANNEL_ACCESS_TOKEN}
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    volumes:
      - ./backend:/app
      - backend_cache:/app/cache
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
      redis:
        condition: service_healthy
    networks:
      - economic-network

  # Celery Worker
  celery-worker:
    build:
      context: ./backend
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: economic-platform-celery-worker
    command: celery -A celery_app worker --loglevel=info --concurrency=4
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://postgres:postgres@postgres:5432/economic_platform
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379/0
      FRED_API_KEY: ${FRED_API_KEY}
      ALPHA_VANTAGE_API_KEY: ${ALPHA_VANTAGE_API_KEY}
      OPENAI_API_KEY: ${OPENAI_API_KEY}
    volumes:
      - ./backend:/app
      - backend_cache:/app/cache
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - redis
    networks:
      - economic-network

  # Celery Beat (スケジューラー)
  celery-beat:
    build:
      context: ./backend
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: economic-platform-celery-beat
    command: celery -A celery_app beat --loglevel=info
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://postgres:postgres@postgres:5432/economic_platform
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379/0
    volumes:
      - ./backend:/app
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - redis
    networks:
      - economic-network

  # Celery Flower (モニタリング)
  celery-flower:
    build:
      context: ./backend
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: economic-platform-celery-flower
    command: celery -A celery_app flower --port=5555
    environment:
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379/0
    ports:
      - "5555:5555"
    depends_on:
      - redis
    networks:
      - economic-network

  # Frontend (React + Vite)
  frontend:
    build:
      context: ./frontend
      dockerfile: Dockerfile.dev
    container_name: economic-platform-frontend
    command: npm run dev
    environment:
      VITE_API_BASE_URL: http://localhost:8000
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    volumes:
      - ./frontend:/app
      - /app/node_modules
    depends_on:
      - backend
    networks:
      - economic-network

networks:
  economic-network:
    driver: bridge

volumes:
  postgres_data:
  redis_data:
  backend_cache:

backend/Dockerfile
FROM python:3.11-slim

WORKDIR /app

# システム依存関係
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    gcc \
    postgresql-client \
    curl \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Python依存関係
COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

# アプリケーションコード
COPY . .

# ポート公開
EXPOSE 8000

# デフォルトコマンド
CMD ["uvicorn", "main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000"]

frontend/Dockerfile.dev
FROM node:20-alpine

WORKDIR /app

# package.jsonとpackage-lock.jsonをコピー
COPY package*.json ./

# 依存関係インストール
RUN npm ci

# アプリケーションコードをコピー
COPY . .

# ポート公開
EXPOSE 3000

# 開発サーバー起動
CMD ["npm", "run", "dev", "--", "--host", "0.0.0.0"]

.env.example

# Redis

# API Keys
