7.1.2 Celeryタスク実装
backend/tasks/calendar_tasks.py
"""
経済指標カレンダー更新タスク
"""
from celery import Task
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from backend.celery_app import celery_app
from backend.services.features.calendar_service import calendar_service
from backend.services.features.notification_service import notification_service

@celery_app.task(
    name="tasks.calendar.fetch_calendar_events",
    bind=True,
    max_retries=3,
    default_retry_delay=300  # 5分後にリトライ
)
def fetch_calendar_events(self: Task):
    """
    経済指標カレンダーを取得
    毎時実行
    """
    try:
        start_date = datetime.now().date()
        end_date = start_date + timedelta(days=14)  # 2週間先まで
        
        result = await calendar_service.fetch_and_update_calendar(
            start_date=start_date,
            end_date=end_date
        )
        
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "updated_count": result["updated_count"],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    except Exception as e:
        self.retry(exc=e)

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.calendar.check_upcoming_events")
def check_upcoming_events():
    """
    直近のイベントをチェックして通知
    15分ごとに実行
    """
    try:
        # 今後1時間以内の高重要度イベントを取得
        upcoming = await calendar_service.get_upcoming_high_impact_events(
            hours=1
        )
        
        # 通知送信
        for event in upcoming:
            await notification_service.send_economic_data_alert(
                indicator_name=event.indicator_name,
                country=event.country,
                actual=event.actual,
                forecast=event.forecast,
                previous=event.previous
            )
        
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "notified_count": len(upcoming)
        }
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error checking upcoming events: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.calendar.update_actual_values")
def update_actual_values():
    """
    実績値を更新
    5分ごとに実行
    """
    try:
        # 今日のイベントで実績値がまだないものを取得
        events = await calendar_service.get_events_pending_actual()
        
        updated_count = 0
        for event in events:
            # APIから実績値を取得
            actual_value = await calendar_service.fetch_actual_value(event)
            
            if actual_value is not None:
                # 実績値を更新
                await calendar_service.update_event_actual(
                    event_id=event.id,
                    actual=actual_value
                )
                
                # サプライズが大きい場合は通知
                if event.forecast and abs((actual_value - event.forecast) / event.forecast) > 0.05:
                    await notification_service.send_economic_data_alert(
                        indicator_name=event.indicator_name,
                        country=event.country,
                        actual=actual_value,
                        forecast=event.forecast,
                        previous=event.previous
                    )
                
                updated_count += 1
        
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "updated_count": updated_count
        }
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error updating actual values: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}

7.1.3 フロントエンド実装（拡張版）
frontend/src/pages/CalendarPage.tsx
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { featuresAPI } from '@/lib/api';
import { format, parseISO, startOfWeek, endOfWeek, eachDayOfInterval } from 'date-fns';
import { CalendarIcon, FunnelIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline';

interface CalendarEvent {
  id: number;
  country: string;
  indicator_name: string;
  release_date: string;
  importance: string;
  forecast: number | null;
  previous: number | null;
  actual: number | null;
  beat_miss: string | null;
  surprise_index: number | null;
}

export const CalendarPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState(new Date());
  const [countryFilter, setCountryFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [importanceFilter, setImportanceFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [view, setView] = useState<'today' | 'week'>('today');

  // データ取得
  const { data, isLoading, refetch } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['calendar-events', view, countryFilter, importanceFilter],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const params: any = {};
      
      if (view === 'week') {
        params.start_date = format(startOfWeek(selectedDate), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
        params.end_date = format(endOfWeek(selectedDate), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
      }
      
      if (countryFilter !== 'all') {
        params.country = countryFilter;
      }
      
      if (importanceFilter !== 'all') {
        params.importance = importanceFilter;
      }
      
      const response = await featuresAPI.getCalendarEvents(params);
      return response.data;
    },
    refetchInterval: 60000, // 1分ごと
  });

  // 国別グループ化
  const eventsByCountry = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data?.events) return {};
    
    return data.events.reduce((acc: any, event: CalendarEvent) => {
      if (!acc[event.country]) {
        acc[event.country] = [];
      }
      acc[event.country].push(event);
      return acc;
    }, {});
  }, [data]);

  // 週表示用のデータ整形
  const eventsByDay = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data?.events || view !== 'week') return {};
    
    const days = eachDayOfInterval({
      start: startOfWeek(selectedDate),
      end: endOfWeek(selectedDate)
    });
    
    const grouped: any = {};
    
    days.forEach(day => {
      const dateStr = format(day, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
      grouped[dateStr] = data.events.filter((event: CalendarEvent) => 
        event.release_date.startsWith(dateStr)
      );
    });
    
    return grouped;
  }, [data, selectedDate, view]);

  const getImportanceBadge = (importance: string) => {
    const colors = {
      high: 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-200',
      medium: 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 border-yellow-200',
      low: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200'
    };
    
    return (
      <span className={`px-2 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded border ${colors[importance as keyof typeof colors]}`}>
        {importance.toUpperCase()}
      </span>
    );
  };

  const getSurpriseIndicator = (event: CalendarEvent) => {
    if (!event.actual || !event.forecast) return null;
    
    const diff = event.actual - event.forecast;
    const diffPercent = (diff / event.forecast) * 100;
    
    if (Math.abs(diffPercent) < 1) return null;
    
    return (
      <span className={`ml-2 text-sm font-bold ${diffPercent > 0 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-600'}`}>
        ({diffPercent > 0 ? '+' : ''}{diffPercent.toFixed(1)}%)
      </span>
    );
  };

  const renderEventCard = (event: CalendarEvent) => (
    <div key={event.id} className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-4 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-3">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 font-bold text-sm">
              {event.country}
            </span>
            {getImportanceBadge(event.importance)}
            <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">
              {format(parseISO(event.release_date), 'HH:mm')}
            </span>
          </div>
          
          <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">
            {event.indicator_name}
          </h3>
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-sm">
            <div>
              <span className="text-gray-500 block mb-1">Forecast</span>
              <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">
                {event.forecast !== null ? event.forecast : '-'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-gray-500 block mb-1">Previous</span>
              <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">
                {event.previous !== null ? event.previous : '-'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-gray-500 block mb-1">Actual</span>
              <span className={`font-bold ${
                event.actual !== null
                  ? event.beat_miss === 'beat' ? 'text-green-600' : event.beat_miss === 'miss' ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-gray-900'
                  : 'text-gray-400'
              }`}>
                {event.actual !== null ? event.actual : 'Pending'}
                {getSurpriseIndicator(event)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  const renderTodayView = () => (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {Object.entries(eventsByCountry).map(([country, events]: [string, any]) => (
        <div key={country}>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-primary-100 text-primary-800 font-bold">
              {country}
            </span>
            <span>{events.length} events</span>
          </h3>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {events.map((event: CalendarEvent) => renderEventCard(event))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );

  const renderWeekView = () => (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-4">
      {Object.entries(eventsByDay).map(([date, events]: [string, any]) => (
        <div key={date} className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-3 min-h-[200px]">
          <div className="text-center mb-3">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
              {format(parseISO(date), 'EEE')}
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">
              {format(parseISO(date), 'd')}
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {events.map((event: CalendarEvent) => (
              <div key={event.id} className="text-xs border-l-2 border-primary-500 pl-2 py-1">
                <div className="font-semibold text-gray-900 truncate">
                  {event.indicator_name}
                </div>
                <div className="text-gray-500">
                  {event.country} - {format(parseISO(event.release_date), 'HH:mm')}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-screen">
        <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-16 w-16 border-b-2 border-primary-600"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="mb-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <CalendarIcon className="w-8 h-8 text-primary-600" />
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">Economic Calendar</h1>
          </div>
          
          <button
            onClick={() => refetch()}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-primary-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-primary-700 transition-colors"
          >
            Refresh
          </button>
        </div>
        
        {/* Filters */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-wrap">
          {/* View Toggle */}
          <div className="flex bg-gray-100 rounded-lg p-1">
            <button
              onClick={() => setView('today')}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-md font-medium transition-colors ${
                view === 'today' ? 'bg-white text-gray-900 shadow' : 'text-gray-600'
              }`}
            >
              Today
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setView('week')}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-md font-medium transition-colors ${
                view === 'week' ? 'bg-white text-gray-900 shadow' : 'text-gray-600'
              }`}
            >
              Week
            </button>
          </div>
          
          {/* Country Filter */}
          <select
            value={countryFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setCountryFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
          >
            <option value="all">All Countries</option>
            <option value="US">United States</option>
            <option value="JP">Japan</option>
            <option value="UK">United Kingdom</option>
            <option value="EU">Euro Area</option>
            <option value="DE">Germany</option>
          </select>
          
          {/* Importance Filter */}
          <select
            value={importanceFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setImportanceFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
          >
            <option value="all">All Importance</option>
            <option value="high">High Impact</option>
            <option value="medium">Medium Impact</option>
            <option value="low">Low Impact</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* Content */}
      {view === 'today' ? renderTodayView() : renderWeekView()}
      
      {/* Empty State */}
      {data?.events?.length === 0 && (
        <div className="text-center py-16">
          <CalendarIcon className="w-16 h-16 mx-auto text-gray-400 mb-4" />
          <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 mb-2">No events found</h3>
          <p className="text-gray-500">Try adjusting your filters</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default CalendarPage;


7.2 機能2: 決算カレンダー
7.2.1 決算データ取得の自動化
backend/tasks/earnings_tasks.py
"""
決算カレンダー更新タスク
"""
from celery import Task
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from backend.celery_app import celery_app
from backend.services.features.earnings_service import earnings_service
from backend.services.features.notification_service import notification_service

@celery_app.task(
    name="tasks.earnings.fetch_earnings_calendar",
    bind=True,
    max_retries=3
)
def fetch_earnings_calendar(self: Task):
    """
    決算カレンダーを取得
    1日1回（市場終了後）実行
    """
    try:
        start_date = datetime.now().date()
        end_date = start_date + timedelta(days=30)
        
        result = await earnings_service.fetch_and_update_earnings(
            start_date=start_date,
            end_date=end_date
        )
        
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "updated_count": result["updated_count"]
        }
    except Exception as e:
        self.retry(exc=e)

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.earnings.check_earnings_results")
def check_earnings_results():
    """
    決算結果をチェックして通知
    市場時間中15分ごとに実行
    """
    try:
        # 今日発表された決算で実績値が更新されたものを取得
        recent_earnings = await earnings_service.get_recent_earnings_with_results(
            hours=1
        )
        
        for earning in recent_earnings:
            # サプライズが大きい場合のみ通知
            if earning.eps_surprise_percent and abs(earning.eps_surprise_percent) > 5:
                await notification_service.send_earnings_alert(
                    symbol=earning.symbol,
                    company_name=earning.company_name,
                    eps_actual=earning.eps_actual,
                    eps_estimate=earning.eps_estimate,
                    surprise_percent=earning.eps_surprise_percent
                )
        
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "processed": len(recent_earnings)
        }
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error checking earnings results: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.earnings.update_symbol_earnings")
def update_symbol_earnings(symbol: str):
    """
    特定銘柄の決算情報を更新
    """
    try:
        result = await earnings_service.fetch_symbol_earnings_detailed(symbol)
        
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "symbol": symbol,
            "data": result
        }
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error updating earnings for {symbol}: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "symbol": symbol, "error": str(e)}


7.3 機能3: ニュース・ヘッドライン統合
7.3.1 複数ソース統合
backend/services/features/news_aggregator.py
"""
ニュース統合サービス
複数ソースからニュースを収集・統合
"""
import asyncio
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from backend.services.features.news_service import news_service
from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client

class NewsAggregator:
    """複数ソースからニュースを統合"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.sources = [
            'rss',      # RSS Feeds
            'twitter',  # Twitter API
            'reddit',   # Reddit API（オプション）
        ]
    
    async def aggregate_news(
        self,
        hours: int = 24,
        categories: List[str] = None
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        複数ソースからニュースを統合
        """
        # キャッシュチェック
        cache_key = f"news:aggregated:{hours}:{','.join(categories or [])}"
        cached = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
        if cached:
            return cached
        
        # 各ソースから並行取得
        tasks = []
        
        if 'rss' in self.sources:
            tasks.append(self._fetch_from_rss(hours, categories))
        
        if 'twitter' in self.sources:
            tasks.append(self._fetch_from_twitter(hours, categories))
        
        if 'reddit' in self.sources:
            tasks.append(self._fetch_from_reddit(hours, categories))
        
        results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
        
        # エラーを除外して統合
        all_articles = []
        for result in results:
            if isinstance(result, list):
                all_articles.extend(result)
        
        # 重複排除（URLベース）
        seen_urls = set()
        unique_articles = []
        
        for article in all_articles:
            if article['url'] not in seen_urls:
                seen_urls.add(article['url'])
                unique_articles.append(article)
        
        # スコアリング（エンゲージメント + 鮮度）
        scored_articles = self._score_articles(unique_articles)
        
        # ソート（スコア降順）
        scored_articles.sort(key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)
        
        # キャッシュ（5分）
        await redis_client.set(cache_key, scored_articles, expire=300)
        
        return scored_articles
    
    async def _fetch_from_rss(
        self,
        hours: int,
        categories: List[str]
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """RSSフィードから取得"""
        return await news_service._fetch_from_rss()
    
    async def _fetch_from_twitter(
        self,
        hours: int,
        categories: List[str]
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Twitterから取得"""
        return await news_service._fetch_from_twitter()
    
    async def _fetch_from_reddit(
        self,
        hours: int,
        categories: List[str]
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Redditから取得（オプション）"""
        # Reddit API実装
        # asyncpraw ライブラリ使用
        return []
    
    def _score_articles(
        self,
        articles: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        記事をスコアリング
        エンゲージメント + 鮮度 + センチメント
        """
        now = datetime.now()
        
        for article in articles:
            score = 0
            
            # エンゲージメントスコア
            engagement = (
                article.get('views', 0) * 0.1 +
                article.get('likes', 0) * 1.0 +
                article.get('shares', 0) * 2.0
            )
            score += min(engagement / 100, 50)  # 最大50点
            
            # 鮮度スコア（24時間以内は高スコア）
            published_at = article.get('published_at')
            if published_at:
                if isinstance(published_at, str):
                    published_at = datetime.fromisoformat(published_at.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
                
                age_hours = (now - published_at).total_seconds() / 3600
                freshness = max(0, 50 - (age_hours * 2))  # 1時間で2点減少
                score += freshness
            
            # センチメントスコア（重要なポジティブニュースを優先）
            sentiment = article.get('sentiment')
            if sentiment == 'positive':
                score += 10
            elif sentiment == 'negative':
                score += 5  # ネガティブも重要
            
            # 重要キーワードボーナス
            important_keywords = ['fed', 'gdp', 'inflation', 'interest rate', 'employment', 'earnings']
            keywords = article.get('keywords', [])
            keyword_bonus = sum(5 for kw in keywords if any(imp in kw.lower() for imp in important_keywords))
            score += min(keyword_bonus, 20)  # 最大20点
            
            article['score'] = score
        
        return articles

# グローバルインスタンス
news_aggregator = NewsAggregator()

7.3.2 ニュースエンドポイント拡張
backend/routers/features/news.py（追加エンドポイント）
@router.get("/aggregated", response_model=NewsResponse)
@cache_response(expire=300)
async def get_aggregated_news(
    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=168),
    categories: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100)
):
    """
    複数ソースから統合されたニュースを取得
    スコアリング済み、重複排除済み
    """
    category_list = categories.split(',') if categories else None
    
    articles = await news_aggregator.aggregate_news(
        hours=hours,
        categories=category_list
    )
    
    # ページネーション
    start = (page - 1) * page_size
    end = start + page_size
    paginated = articles[start:end]
    
    return {
        "articles": paginated,
        "total_count": len(articles),
        "page": page,
        "page_size": page_size
    }

@router.get("/related/{symbol}")
async def get_related_news(
    symbol: str,
    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=168),
    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100)
):
    """
    特定銘柄に関連するニュースを取得
    AI による関連性判定付き
    """
    # 銘柄名取得
    company_name = await get_company_name(symbol)
    
    # ニュース検索
    all_news = await news_aggregator.aggregate_news(hours=hours)
    
    # 関連性フィルタリング
    related = []
    for article in all_news:
        if symbol.upper() in article.get('symbols', []):
            article['relevance'] = 1.0
            related.append(article)
        elif company_name and company_name.lower() in article['title'].lower():
            article['relevance'] = 0.9
            related.append(article)
        elif company_name and company_name.lower() in (article.get('summary') or '').lower():
            article['relevance'] = 0.7
            related.append(article)
    
    # 関連性でソート
    related.sort(key=lambda x: x['relevance'], reverse=True)
    
    return {
        "symbol": symbol,
        "company_name": company_name,
        "articles": related[:limit],
        "total_found": len(related)
    }
7.4 機能4: AI分析とインサイト生成
7.4.1 高度なAI分析パイプライン
backend/services/features/ai_pipeline.py
"""
AI分析パイプライン
複数の分析を組み合わせた高度なインサイト生成
"""
import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import numpy as np
from scipy import stats

from backend.services.features.ai_analysis_service import ai_analysis_service
from backend.core.redis_client import redis_client

class AIAnalysisPipeline:
    """AI分析パイプライン"""
    
    async def generate_comprehensive_report(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifier: str,
        time_range_days: int = 90
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        包括的な分析レポートを生成
        
        含まれる分析:
        1. トレンド分析
        2. 季節性分析
        3. 異常検出
        4. 予測分析
        5. 相関分析
        6. AIテキスト生成
        """
        # キャッシュチェック
        cache_key = f"ai:comprehensive:{data_type}:{identifier}:{time_range_days}"
        cached = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
        if cached:
            return cached
        
        # データ取得
        data = await ai_analysis_service._fetch_data(data_type, identifier, time_range_days)
        
        if not data or len(data) < 30:
            return {"error": "Insufficient data for comprehensive analysis"}
        
        # 並行して複数の分析を実行
        analyses = await asyncio.gather(
            self._trend_analysis(data),
            self._seasonality_analysis(data),
            self._anomaly_detection(data),
            self._forecast_analysis(data),
            self._volatility_analysis(data),
            return_exceptions=True
        )
        
        trend_result, seasonality_result, anomaly_result, forecast_result, volatility_result = analyses
        
        # 統計サマリー
        stats_summary = self._calculate_comprehensive_stats(data)
        
        # AIテキスト生成
        ai_narrative = await self._generate_narrative(
            data_type=data_type,
            identifier=identifier,
            data=data,
            trend=trend_result,
            seasonality=seasonality_result,
            anomaly=anomaly_result,
            forecast=forecast_result,
            volatility=volatility_result,
            stats=stats_summary
        )
        
        # 投資シグナル生成
        signals = self._generate_trading_signals(
            trend=trend_result,
            forecast=forecast_result,
            volatility=volatility_result
        )
        
        report = {
            "data_type": data_type,
            "identifier": identifier,
            "analysis_period": {
                "start": data[0]["date"],
                "end": data[-1]["date"],
                "data_points": len(data)
            },
            "statistics": stats_summary,
            "trend_analysis": trend_result,
            "seasonality_analysis": seasonality_result,
            "anomaly_detection": anomaly_result,
            "forecast": forecast_result,
            "volatility_analysis": volatility_result,
            "trading_signals": signals,
            "ai_narrative": ai_narrative,
            "confidence_score": self._calculate_overall_confidence(analyses),
            "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # キャッシュ（6時間）
        await redis_client.set(cache_key, report, expire=21600)
        
        return report
    
    async def _trend_analysis(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """詳細なトレンド分析"""
        values = np.array([d["value"] for d in data])
        x = np.arange(len(values))
        
        # 線形回帰
        slope, intercept, r_value, p_value, std_err = stats.linregress(x, values)
        
        # 移動平均
        ma_20 = np.convolve(values, np.ones(20)/20, mode='valid')
        ma_50 = np.convolve(values, np.ones(min(50, len(values)))/min(50, len(values)), mode='valid')
        
        # トレンド強度分類
        r_squared = r_value ** 2
        if r_squared > 0.7:
            strength = "strong"
        elif r_squared > 0.4:
            strength = "moderate"
        else:
            strength = "weak"
        
        # トレンド方向
        if slope > 0.01:
            direction = "upward"
            momentum = "bullish"
        elif slope < -0.01:
            direction = "downward"
            momentum = "bearish"
        else:
            direction = "sideways"
            momentum = "neutral"
        
        # 加速度分析（トレンドの変化率）
        recent_slope, _, _, _, _ = stats.linregress(x[-30:], values[-30:])
        acceleration = recent_slope - slope
        
        return {
            "direction": direction,
            "strength": strength,
            "momentum": momentum,
            "slope": float(slope),
            "r_squared": float(r_squared),
            "p_value": float(p_value),
            "acceleration": float(acceleration),
            "is_accelerating": acceleration > 0 if direction == "upward" else acceleration < 0,
            "moving_averages": {
                "ma_20": float(ma_20[-1]) if len(ma_20) > 0 else None,
                "ma_50": float(ma_50[-1]) if len(ma_50) > 0 else None,
                "golden_cross": bool(len(ma_20) > 0 and len(ma_50) > 0 and ma_20[-1] > ma_50[-1])
            }
        }
    
    async def _seasonality_analysis(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """季節性分析"""
        if len(data) < 24:  # 最低2年分のデータが必要
            return {"detected": False, "reason": "insufficient_data"}
        
        values = np.array([d["value"] for d in data])
        
        # 月次パターン検出
        monthly_avg = {}
        for i, d in enumerate(data):
            try:
                month = datetime.fromisoformat(d["date"]).month
                if month not in monthly_avg:
                    monthly_avg[month] = []
                monthly_avg[month].append(values[i])
            except:
                continue
        
        # 各月の平均を計算
        monthly_pattern = {
            month: np.mean(vals) for month, vals in monthly_avg.items()
        }
        
        # 季節性の強度（変動係数）
        if monthly_pattern:
            pattern_values = list(monthly_pattern.values())
            seasonality_strength = np.std(pattern_values) / np.mean(pattern_values)
            
            has_seasonality = seasonality_strength > 0.1  # 10%以上の変動
        else:
            has_seasonality = False
            seasonality_strength = 0
        
        # ピーク月とトラフ月
        if monthly_pattern:
            peak_month = max(monthly_pattern, key=monthly_pattern.get)
            trough_month = min(monthly_pattern, key=monthly_pattern.get)
        else:
            peak_month = None
            trough_month = None
        
        return {
            "detected": has_seasonality,
            "strength": float(seasonality_strength),
            "monthly_pattern": {str(k): float(v) for k, v in monthly_pattern.items()},
            "peak_month": peak_month,
            "trough_month": trough_month,
            "interpretation": self._interpret_seasonality(has_seasonality, seasonality_strength)
        }
    
    def _interpret_seasonality(self, has_seasonality: bool, strength: float) -> str:
        """季節性の解釈"""
        if not has_seasonality:
            return "No significant seasonal pattern detected"
        elif strength > 0.3:
            return "Strong seasonal pattern - significant variation across months"
        elif strength > 0.15:
            return "Moderate seasonal pattern - noticeable variation across months"
        else:
            return "Weak seasonal pattern - slight variation across months"
    
    async def _anomaly_detection(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """高度な異常検出"""
        values = np.array([d["value"] for d in data])
        
        # Z-scoreベース
        mean = np.mean(values)
        std = np.std(values)
        z_scores = (values - mean) / std if std > 0 else np.zeros_like(values)
        
        # IQRベース（外れ値検出）
        q1 = np.percentile(values, 25)
        q3 = np.percentile(values, 75)
        iqr = q3 - q1
        lower_bound = q1 - 1.5 * iqr
        upper_bound = q3 + 1.5 * iqr
        
        anomalies = []
        for i, d in enumerate(data):
            z_score = z_scores[i]
            is_outlier = values[i] < lower_bound or values[i] > upper_bound
            
            if abs(z_score) > 2.5 or is_outlier:
                anomalies.append({
                    "date": d["date"],
                    "value": float(values[i]),
                    "z_score": float(z_score),
                    "deviation_percent": float((values[i] - mean) / mean * 100),
                    "severity": "high" if abs(z_score) > 3 or is_outlier else "medium"
                })
        
        # 異常クラスター検出（連続した異常）
        clusters = self._detect_anomaly_clusters(anomalies)
        
        return {
            "total_anomalies": len(anomalies),
            "anomalies": anomalies[-10:],  # 最新10件
            "clusters": clusters,
            "anomaly_rate": len(anomalies) / len(data) * 100,
            "interpretation": self._interpret_anomalies(len(anomalies), len(data))
        }
    
    def _detect_anomaly_clusters(self, anomalies: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """異常のクラスター検出"""
        if len(anomalies) < 2:
            return []
        
        clusters = []
        current_cluster = [anomalies[0]]
        
        for i in range(1, len(anomalies)):
            # 日付が連続または近い場合はクラスター化
            prev_date = datetime.fromisoformat(anomalies[i-1]["date"])
            curr_date = datetime.fromisoformat(anomalies[i]["date"])
            
            if (curr_date - prev_date).days <= 7:  # 7日以内
                current_cluster.append(anomalies[i])
            else:
                if len(current_cluster) >= 3:  # 3つ以上でクラスター
                    clusters.append({
                        "start_date": current_cluster[0]["date"],
                        "end_date": current_cluster[-1]["date"],
                        "count": len(current_cluster),
                        "avg_severity": np.mean([a["z_score"] for a in current_cluster])
                    })
                current_cluster = [anomalies[i]]
        
        # 最後のクラスター
        if len(current_cluster) >= 3:
            clusters.append({
                "start_date": current_cluster[0]["date"],
                "end_date": current_cluster[-1]["date"],
                "count": len(current_cluster),
                "avg_severity": np.mean([a["z_score"] for a in current_cluster])
            })
        
        return clusters
    
    def _interpret_anomalies(self, anomaly_count: int, total_points: int) -> str:
        """異常の解釈"""
        rate = anomaly_count / total_points * 100
        
        if rate < 2:
            return "Very stable - minimal anomalies detected"
        elif rate < 5:
            return "Stable - few anomalies within normal range"
        elif rate < 10:
            return "Moderate volatility - some significant deviations"
        else:
            return "High volatility - frequent anomalies detected"
    
    async def _forecast_analysis(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """予測分析（Simple Moving Average + 線形外挿）"""
        values = np.array([d["value"] for d in data])
        x = np.arange(len(values))
        
        # 線形回帰で予測
        slope, intercept, r_value, _, _ = stats.linregress(x, values)
        
        # 次の期間（1-3期先）を予測
        forecast_periods = 3
        predictions = []
        
        for i in range(1, forecast_periods + 1):
            predicted_value = slope * (len(values) + i) + intercept
            predictions.append({
                "period": i,
                "value": float(predicted_value),
                "confidence": float(r_value ** 2)  # R²を信頼度として使用
            })
        
        # トレンド継続確率
        recent_trend = slope
        trend_consistency = r_value ** 2
        
        continuation_probability = trend_consistency * 100
        
        return {
            "predictions": predictions,
            "forecast_trend": "upward" if slope > 0 else "downward" if slope < 0 else "flat",
            "continuation_probability": float(continuation_probability),
            "confidence_level": "high" if continuation_probability > 70 else "medium" if continuation_probability > 40 else "low",
            "methodology": "Linear regression with historical trend analysis"
        }
    
    async def _volatility_analysis(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ボラティリティ分析"""
        values = np.array([d["value"] for d in data])
        
        # リターン計算
        returns = np.diff(values) / values[:-1] * 100
        
        # ボラティリティ指標
        volatility = np.std(returns)
        
        # ボラティリティ分類
        if volatility < 1:
            classification = "very_low"
            description = "Very stable with minimal fluctuations"
        elif volatility < 2:
            classification = "low"
            description = "Relatively stable with moderate fluctuations"
        elif volatility < 5:
            classification = "moderate"
            description = "Moderate volatility with noticeable swings"
        elif volatility < 10:
            classification = "high"
            description = "High volatility with significant swings"
        else:
            classification = "very_high"
            description = "Very high volatility with extreme swings"
        
        # 最近のボラティリティ（30期間）
        recent_returns = returns[-30:]
        recent_volatility = np.std(recent_returns)
        
        volatility_trend = "increasing" if recent_volatility > volatility else "decreasing"
        
        return {
            "overall_volatility": float(volatility),
            "recent_volatility": float(recent_volatility),
            "classification": classification,
            "description": description,
            "volatility_trend": volatility_trend,
            "max_drawdown": float(np.min(returns)),
            "max_gain": float(np.max(returns))
        }
    
    def _calculate_comprehensive_stats(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """包括的な統計サマリー"""
        values = np.array([d["value"] for d in data])
        
        return {
            "count": len(values),
            "mean": float(np.mean(values)),
            "median": float(np.median(values)),
            "std_dev": float(np.std(values)),
            "variance": float(np.var(values)),
            "min": float(np.min(values)),
            "max": float(np.max(values)),
            "range": float(np.max(values) - np.min(values)),
            "q1": float(np.percentile(values, 25)),
            "q3": float(np.percentile(values, 75)),
            "iqr": float(np.percentile(values, 75) - np.percentile(values, 25)),
            "skewness": float(stats.skew(values)),
            "kurtosis": float(stats.kurtosis(values)),
            "coefficient_of_variation": float(np.std(values) / np.mean(values)) if np.mean(values) != 0 else 0
        }
    
    def _generate_trading_signals(
        self,
        trend: Dict[str, Any],
        forecast: Dict[str, Any],
        volatility: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """トレーディングシグナル生成"""
        
        # シグナルスコア計算
        score = 0
        signals = []
        
        # トレンドベースのシグナル
        if trend["direction"] == "upward" and trend["strength"] in ["strong", "moderate"]:
            score += 30
            signals.append("Strong upward trend detected")
        elif trend["direction"] == "downward" and trend["strength"] in ["strong", "moderate"]:
            score -= 30
            signals.append("Strong downward trend detected")
        
        # ゴールデンクロス/デスクロス
        if trend["moving_averages"]["golden_cross"]:
            score += 20
            signals.append("Golden Cross - bullish signal")
        
        # 予測ベースのシグナル
        if forecast["continuation_probability"] > 70:
            if forecast["forecast_trend"] == "upward":
                score += 20
                signals.append("High probability of upward continuation")
            else:
                score -= 20
                signals.append("High probability of downward continuation")
        
        # ボラティリティ警告
        if volatility["classification"] in ["high", "very_high"]:
            signals.append("Caution: High volatility detected")
        
        # 総合判定
        if score > 40:
            overall = "strong_buy"
        elif score > 20:
            overall = "buy"
        elif score > -20:
            overall = "hold"
        elif score > -40:
            overall = "sell"
        else:
            overall = "strong_sell"
        
        return {
            "overall_signal": overall,
            "score": score,
            "signals": signals,
            "confidence": "high" if abs(score) > 40 else "medium" if abs(score) > 20 else "low",
            "disclaimer": "For informational purposes only. Not financial advice."
        }
    
    async def _generate_narrative(
        self,
        data_type: str,
        identifier: str,
        data: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        **analyses
    ) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """AI生成のナラティブ"""
        
        # 簡易版プロンプト構築
        prompt = f"""
Analyze {data_type} {identifier} based on the following findings:

Trend: {analyses['trend']['direction']} ({analyses['trend']['strength']})
Seasonality: {analyses['seasonality']['interpretation']}
Volatility: {analyses['volatility']['description']}
Forecast: {analyses['forecast']['forecast_trend']} with {analyses['forecast']['confidence_level']} confidence

Provide:
1. Executive Summary (2-3 sentences)
2. Key Insights (3-5 bullet points)
3. Risk Assessment (1-2 sentences)

Format as JSON with keys: summary, insights (array), risk_assessment
"""
        
        # AI生成（OpenAI or Anthropic）
        try:
            ai_response = await ai_analysis_service._call_openai(prompt)
        except:
            # フォールバック
            ai_response = {
                "summary": f"{identifier} shows {analyses['trend']['direction']} trend with {analyses['volatility']['classification']} volatility.",
                "insights": [
                    f"Trend: {analyses['trend']['direction']} ({analyses['trend']['strength']})",
                    f"Volatility: {analyses['volatility']['classification']}",
                    f"Forecast: {analyses['forecast']['forecast_trend']}"
                ],
                "risk_assessment": f"Volatility is {analyses['volatility']['classification']}, caution advised."
            }
        
        return ai_response
    
    def _calculate_overall_confidence(self, analyses: List) -> float:
        """全体の信頼度スコア"""
        # エラーがない分析の数
        valid_count = sum(1 for a in analyses if not isinstance(a, Exception))
        confidence = valid_count / len(analyses)
        
        return round(confidence, 2)

# グローバルインスタンス
ai_pipeline = AIAnalysisPipeline()

@router.post("/comprehensive-report", response_model=dict)
async def generate_comprehensive_report(
    data_type: str = Body(...),
    identifier: str = Body(...),
    time_range_days: int = Body(90, ge=30, le=365)
):
    """
    包括的AI分析レポートを生成
    
    含む分析:
    - トレンド分析
    - 季節性分析
    - 異常検出
    - 予測分析
    - ボラティリティ分析
    - トレーディングシグナル
    - AIナラティブ
    """
    from backend.services.features.ai_pipeline import ai_pipeline
    
    try:
        report = await ai_pipeline.generate_comprehensive_report(
            data_type=data_type,
            identifier=identifier,
            time_range_days=time_range_days
        )
        
        return report
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error generating report: {str(e)}")

@router.get("/market-sentiment")
@cache_response(expire=3600)
async def get_market_sentiment():
    """
    市場全体のセンチメント分析
    主要指標のAI分析を統合
    """
    from backend.services.features.ai_pipeline import ai_pipeline
    
    # 主要指標
    indicators = [
        {"type": "stock", "id": "^GSPC", "name": "S&P 500"},
        {"type": "stock", "id": "^DJI", "name": "Dow Jones"},
        {"type": "forex", "id": "USD/JPY", "name": "USD/JPY"},
        {"type": "commodity", "id": "gold", "name": "Gold"}
    ]
    
    sentiments = []
    
    for indicator in indicators:
        try:
            analysis = await ai_analysis_service.generate_analysis(
                data_type=indicator["type"],
                identifier=indicator["id"],
                time_range_days=30,
                analysis_type="trend"
            )
            
            sentiments.append({
                "name": indicator["name"],
                "trend": analysis["trend_direction"],
                "momentum": "bullish" if analysis["trend_direction"] == "upward" else "bearish" if analysis["trend_direction"] == "downward" else "neutral"
            })
        except:
            continue
    
    # 総合センチメント計算
    bullish_count = sum(1 for s in sentiments if s["momentum"] == "bullish")
    bearish_count = sum(1 for s in sentiments if s["momentum"] == "bearish")
    
    if bullish_count > bearish_count:
        overall = "bullish"
    elif bearish_count > bullish_count:
        overall = "bearish"
    else:
        overall = "neutral"
    
    return {
        "overall_sentiment": overall,
        "indicators": sentiments,
        "bullish_count": bullish_count,
        "bearish_count": bearish_count,
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
    }

"""
通知テンプレートシステム
様々な通知タイプに対応した柔軟なテンプレート
"""
from typing import Dict, Any
from datetime import datetime

class NotificationTemplates:
    """通知テンプレート管理"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def format_economic_alert(
        indicator_name: str,
        country: str,
        actual: float,
        forecast: float = None,
        previous: float = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """経済指標アラートのフォーマット"""
        
        # サプライズ計算
        surprise_text = ""
        if forecast is not None:
            diff = actual - forecast
            diff_percent = (diff / forecast * 100) if forecast != 0 else 0
            
            if abs(diff_percent) > 10:
                emoji = "🚨"
                surprise_text = f" {emoji} 大幅サプライズ ({diff_percent:+.1f}%)"
            elif diff > 0:
                emoji = "✅"
                surprise_text = f" {emoji} 予想上回る ({diff_percent:+.1f}%)"
            else:
                emoji = "⚠️"
                surprise_text = f" {emoji} 予想下回る ({diff_percent:+.1f}%)"
        
        # メッセージ本文
        message = f"""
📊 **経済指標発表**

**{country} - {indicator_name}**

実績値: **{actual}**{surprise_text}
予想値: {forecast if forecast is not None else 'N/A'}
前回値: {previous if previous is not None else 'N/A'}

📅 {datetime.now().strftime('%Y年%m月%d日 %H:%M')}
"""
        
        # Discord Embed
        embed = {
            "title": f"📊 {country} - {indicator_name}",
            "description": f"実績: **{actual}**{surprise_text}",
            "color": 0x00ff00 if (forecast and actual > forecast) else 0xff0000 if (forecast and actual < forecast) else 0x3498db,
            "fields": [
                {"name": "予想", "value": str(forecast) if forecast else "N/A", "inline": True},
                {"name": "前回", "value": str(previous) if previous else "N/A", "inline": True},
                {"name": "サプライズ", "value": surprise_text or "N/A", "inline": True}
            ],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "footer": {"text": "Economic Data Platform"}
        }
        
        return {"message": message, "embed": embed}
    
    @staticmethod
    def format_earnings_alert(
        symbol: str,
        company_name: str,
        eps_actual: float,
        eps_estimate: float = None,
        revenue_actual: float = None,
        revenue_estimate: float = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """決算アラートのフォーマット"""
        
        eps_surprise = ""
        if eps_estimate is not None:
            surprise_pct = ((eps_actual - eps_estimate) / eps_estimate * 100) if eps_estimate != 0 else 0
            
            if surprise_pct > 5:
                eps_surprise = f" ✅ +{surprise_pct:.1f}%"
            elif surprise_pct < -5:
                eps_surprise = f" ⚠️ {surprise_pct:.1f}%"
            else:
                eps_surprise = f" 📊 {surprise_pct:+.1f}%"
        
        message = f"""
📈 **決算発表**

**{symbol} - {company_name}**

💰 EPS実績: **${eps_actual:.2f}**{eps_surprise}
   EPS予想: ${eps_estimate:.2f if eps_estimate else 'N/A'}
"""
        
        if revenue_actual:
            message += f"\n📊 売上実績: ${revenue_actual:.2f}M"
            if revenue_estimate:
                message += f"\n   売上予想: ${revenue_estimate:.2f}M"
        
        message += f"\n\n📅 {datetime.now().strftime('%Y年%m月%d日 %H:%M')}"
        
        embed = {
            "title": f"📈 {symbol} - 決算発表",
            "description": f"EPS: **${eps_actual:.2f}**{eps_surprise}",
            "color": 0x00ff00 if (eps_estimate and eps_actual > eps_estimate) else 0xff0000,
            "fields": [
                {"name": "EPS予想", "value": f"${eps_estimate:.2f}" if eps_estimate else "N/A", "inline": True},
                {"name": "EPS実績", "value": f"${eps_actual:.2f}", "inline": True}
            ],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        return {"message": message, "embed": embed}
    
    @staticmethod
    def format_ai_insight_alert(
        identifier: str,
        data_type: str,
        summary: str,
        key_insights: list,
        signal: str = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """AI分析アラートのフォーマット"""
        
        insights_text = "\n".join([f"• {insight}" for insight in key_insights[:3]])
        
        signal_emoji = {
            "strong_buy": "🟢🟢",
            "buy": "🟢",
            "hold": "⚪",
            "sell": "🔴",
            "strong_sell": "🔴🔴"
        }.get(signal, "")
        
        message = f"""
🤖 **AI分析レポート**

**{identifier}** ({data_type})

{summary}

**主要インサイト:**
{insights_text}
"""
        
        if signal:
            message += f"\n\n**シグナル:** {signal_emoji} {signal.upper()}"
        
        message += f"\n\n📅 {datetime.now().strftime('%Y年%m月%d日 %H:%M')}"
        
        embed = {
            "title": f"🤖 AI分析: {identifier}",
            "description": summary,
            "color": 0x9b59b6,
            "fields": [
                {"name": "データタイプ", "value": data_type, "inline": True},
                {"name": "シグナル", "value": f"{signal_emoji} {signal}" if signal else "N/A", "inline": True}
            ],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        return {"message": message, "embed": embed}
    
    @staticmethod
    def format_daily_summary(
        market_performance: Dict[str, Any],
        top_movers: list,
        economic_events: int
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """日次サマリーのフォーマット"""
        
        message = f"""
📊 **デイリーマーケットサマリー**

**主要指標:**
"""
        
        for index_name, data in market_performance.items():
            change_emoji = "📈" if data["change"] > 0 else "📉"
            message += f"{change_emoji} {index_name}: {data['close']:.2f} ({data['change']:+.2f}%)\n"
        
        if top_movers:
            message += "\n**🔥 注目銘柄:**\n"
            for mover in top_movers[:3]:
                message += f"• {mover['symbol']}: {mover['change']:+.2f}%\n"
        
        message += f"\n📅 今日の経済イベント: {economic_events}件"
        message += f"\n\n{datetime.now().strftime('%Y年%m月%d日')}"
        
        embed = {
            "title": "📊 デイリーマーケットサマリー",
            "color": 0x3498db,
            "fields": [
                {"name": "経済イベント", "value": f"{economic_events}件", "inline": True}
            ],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        return {"message": message, "embed": embed}

# グローバルインスタンス
notification_templates = NotificationTemplates()

backend/tasks/notification_tasks.py
"""
通知タスク
定期的な通知送信
"""
from celery import Task
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from backend.celery_app import celery_app
from backend.services.features.notification_service import notification_service
from backend.services.features.notification_templates import notification_templates
from backend.services.features.calendar_service import calendar_service
from backend.services.features.earnings_service import earnings_service
from backend.services.markets.yfinance_service import yfinance_service

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.notifications.send_daily_summary")
def send_daily_summary():
    """
    デイリーマーケットサマリーを送信
    """
    try:
        # 主要指標のパフォーマンス取得
        indices = ["^GSPC", "^DJI", "^IXIC"]
        market_performance = {}
        
        for index in indices:
            data = await yfinance_service.fetch_quote(index)
            if data:
                market_performance[data['name']] = {
                    'close': data['price'],
                    'change': data['change_percent']
                }
        
        # トップムーバー取得（例：S&P 500構成銘柄から）
        # 実装省略
        top_movers = []
        
        # 今日の経済イベント数
        today_events = await calendar_service.get_today_events_count()
        
        # テンプレート生成
        notification_data = notification_templates.format_daily_summary(
            market_performance=market_performance,
            top_movers=top_movers,
            economic_events=today_events
        )
        
        # サブスクライバーに送信
        await notification_service._send_to_subscribers(
            notification_type="daily_summary",
            message=notification_data["message"],
            embed=notification_data["embed"]
        )
        
        return {"status": "success", "sent_at": datetime.now().isoformat()}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error sending daily summary: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.notifications.send_weekly_report")
def send_weekly_report():
    """
    週次レポートを送信
    """
    try:
        # 週のサマリーデータ取得
        start_of_week = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=7)
        
        # 主要指標の週間パフォーマンス
        # AIによる週次分析
        # 来週の注目イベント
        
        message = """
📊 **週次マーケットレポート**

**今週のハイライト:**
• S&P 500: +2.3%
• 日経平均: +1.8%
• 重要イベント: 5件

**来週の注目:**
• 米国雇用統計（金曜日）
• 日銀金融政策決定会合（木曜日）

詳細はアプリで確認
"""
        
        await notification_service._send_to_subscribers(

7.6 機能6: リアルタイム更新システム
7.6.1 WebSocket実装
backend/routers/websocket.py
"""
WebSocketエンドポイント
リアルタイムデータ配信
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from typing import Dict, Set
import asyncio
import json
from datetime import datetime

router = APIRouter()

# アクティブな接続管理
class ConnectionManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.active_connections: Dict[str, Set[WebSocket]] = {}
        self.user_subscriptions: Dict[WebSocket, Set[str]] = {}
    
    async def connect(self, websocket: WebSocket):
        await websocket.accept()
        self.user_subscriptions[websocket] = set()
    
    def disconnect(self, websocket: WebSocket):
        if websocket in self.user_subscriptions:
            # 全てのチャンネルから削除
            for channel in self.user_subscriptions[websocket]:
                if channel in self.active_connections:
                    self.active_connections[channel].discard(websocket)
            del self.user_subscriptions[websocket]
    
    async def subscribe(self, websocket: WebSocket, channel: str):
        """チャンネルに登録"""
        if channel not in self.active_connections:
            self.active_connections[channel] = set()
        
        self.active_connections[channel].add(websocket)
        self.user_subscriptions[websocket].add(channel)
        
        await websocket.send_json({
            "type": "subscribed",
            "channel": channel,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
    
    async def unsubscribe(self, websocket: WebSocket, channel: str):
        """チャンネルから削除"""
        if channel in self.active_connections:
            self.active_connections[channel].discard(websocket)
        
        if websocket in self.user_subscriptions:
            self.user_subscriptions[websocket].discard(channel)
        
        await websocket.send_json({
            "type": "unsubscribed",
            "channel": channel,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
    
    async def broadcast_to_channel(self, channel: str, message: dict):
        """特定チャンネルにブロードキャスト"""
        if channel not in self.active_connections:
            return
        
        disconnected = set()
        
        for connection in self.active_connections[channel]:
            try:
                await connection.send_json(message)
            except Exception:
                disconnected.add(connection)
        
        # 切断された接続を削除
        for connection in disconnected:
            self.disconnect(connection)

manager = ConnectionManager()

@router.websocket("/ws")
async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket):
    """
    WebSocketエンドポイント
    
    クライアントからのメッセージ形式:
    {
        "action": "subscribe" | "unsubscribe",
        "channel": "market:AAPL" | "calendar:today" | "news:latest"
    }
    """
    await manager.connect(websocket)
    
    try:
        while True:
            # クライアントからのメッセージを受信
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            message = json.loads(data)
            
            action = message.get("action")
            channel = message.get("channel")
            
            if action == "subscribe" and channel:
                await manager.subscribe(websocket, channel)
            
            elif action == "unsubscribe" and channel:
                await manager.unsubscribe(websocket, channel)
            
            elif action == "ping":
                await websocket.send_json({
                    "type": "pong",
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                })
    
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        manager.disconnect(websocket)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"WebSocket error: {e}")
        manager.disconnect(websocket)

# データ更新時にブロードキャスト
async def broadcast_market_update(symbol: str, data: dict):
    """市場データ更新をブロードキャスト"""
    channel = f"market:{symbol}"
    message = {
        "type": "market_update",
        "channel": channel,
        "data": data,
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
    }
    await manager.broadcast_to_channel(channel, message)

async def broadcast_calendar_update(event_data: dict):
    """経済カレンダー更新をブロードキャスト"""
    channel = "calendar:updates"
    message = {
        "type": "calendar_update",
        "channel": channel,
        "data": event_data,
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
    }
    await manager.broadcast_to_channel(channel, message)

async def broadcast_news_update(article: dict):
    """ニュース更新をブロードキャスト"""
    channel = "news:latest"
    message = {
        "type": "news_update",
        "channel": channel,
        "data": article,
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
    }
    await manager.broadcast_to_channel(channel, message)

"""
リアルタイム更新タスク
WebSocket経由でクライアントに配信
"""
from celery import Task
from backend.celery_app import celery_app
from backend.routers.websocket import broadcast_market_update, broadcast_calendar_update, broadcast_news_update
from backend.services.markets.yfinance_service import yfinance_service

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.realtime.update_market_data")
def update_market_data():
    """
    市場データを更新してブロードキャスト
    1分ごとに実行
    """
    try:
        # 主要銘柄リスト
        symbols = ["AAPL", "MSFT", "GOOGL", "AMZN", "TSLA", "^GSPC", "^DJI"]
        
        for symbol in symbols:
            try:
                # 最新データ取得
                quote = await yfinance_service.fetch_quote(symbol)
                
                if quote:
                    # WebSocketでブロードキャスト
                    await broadcast_market_update(symbol, quote)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error updating {symbol}: {e}")
                continue
        
        return {"status": "success", "updated_symbols": len(symbols)}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error in market data update: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.realtime.check_calendar_updates")
def check_calendar_updates():
    """
    経済カレンダーの更新をチェックしてブロードキャスト
    5分ごとに実行
    """
    try:
        # 最近更新されたイベント（実績値が追加されたもの）
        from backend.services.features.calendar_service import calendar_service
        
        recent_updates = await calendar_service.get_recent_updates(minutes=5)
        
        for event in recent_updates:
            await broadcast_calendar_update({
                "id": event.id,
                "country": event.country,
                "indicator_name": event.indicator_name,
                "actual": event.actual,
                "forecast": event.forecast,
                "beat_miss": event.beat_miss
            })
        
        return {"status": "success", "updates": len(recent_updates)}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error checking calendar updates: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}

@celery_app.task(name="tasks.realtime.stream_news_updates")
def stream_news_updates():
    """
    最新ニュースをストリーム配信
    2分ごとに実行
    """
    try:
        from backend.services.features.news_service import news_service
        
        # 最新のニュース（過去5分）
        recent_news = await news_service.get_recent_articles(minutes=5)
        
        for article in recent_news:
            await broadcast_news_update({
                "id": article.id,
                "title": article.title,
                "source": article.source,
                "url": article.url,
                "published_at": article.published_at.isoformat(),
                "sentiment": article.sentiment
            })
        
        return {"status": "success", "articles": len(recent_news)}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error streaming news: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}

7.6.3 フロントエンドWebSocket統合
frontend/src/hooks/useWebSocket.ts
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

interface WebSocketMessage {
  type: string;
  channel: string;
  data?: any;
  timestamp: string;
}

export const useWebSocket = (url: string) => {
  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false);
  const [lastMessage, setLastMessage] = useState<WebSocketMessage | null>(null);
  const ws = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const reconnectTimeout = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout>();
  const subscriptions = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());

  useEffect(() => {
    connect();

    return () => {
      if (ws.current) {
        ws.current.close();
      }
      if (reconnectTimeout.current) {
        clearTimeout(reconnectTimeout.current);
      }
    };
  }, [url]);

  const connect = () => {
    try {
      ws.current = new WebSocket(url);

      ws.current.onopen = () => {
        console.log('WebSocket connected');
        setIsConnected(true);
        
        // 既存のサブスクリプションを再登録
        subscriptions.current.forEach(channel => {
          subscribe(channel);
        });
      };

      ws.current.onmessage = (event) => {
        try {
          const message: WebSocketMessage = JSON.parse(event.data);
          setLastMessage(message);
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Error parsing WebSocket message:', error);
        }
      };

      ws.current.onerror = (error) => {
        console.error('WebSocket error:', error);
      };

      ws.current.onclose = () => {
        console.log('WebSocket disconnected');
        setIsConnected(false);
        
        // 5秒後に再接続
        reconnectTimeout.current = setTimeout(() => {
          console.log('Reconnecting...');
          connect();
        }, 5000);
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error connecting to WebSocket:', error);
    }
  };

  const subscribe = (channel: string) => {
    if (ws.current && ws.current.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      ws.current.send(JSON.stringify({
        action: 'subscribe',
        channel: channel
      }));
      subscriptions.current.add(channel);
    }
  };

  const unsubscribe = (channel: string) => {
    if (ws.current && ws.current.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      ws.current.send(JSON.stringify({
        action: 'unsubscribe',
        channel: channel
      }));
      subscriptions.current.delete(channel);
    }
  };

  const send = (message: any) => {
    if (ws.current && ws.current.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      ws.current.send(JSON.stringify(message));
    }
  };

  return {
    isConnected,
    lastMessage,
    subscribe,
    unsubscribe,
    send
  };
};

frontend/src/components/features/RealTimeMarketTicker.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useWebSocket } from '@/hooks/useWebSocket';

interface MarketQuote {
  symbol: string;
  price: number;
  change: number;
  change_percent: number;
}

export const RealTimeMarketTicker: React.FC = () => {
  const WS_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_WS_URL || 'ws://localhost:8000/ws';
  const { isConnected, lastMessage, subscribe, unsubscribe } = useWebSocket(WS_URL);
  const [quotes, setQuotes] = useState<Map<string, MarketQuote>>(new Map());

  const symbols = ['AAPL', 'MSFT', 'GOOGL', 'AMZN', 'TSLA', '^GSPC', '^DJI'];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isConnected) {
      // 各シンボルをサブスクライブ
      symbols.forEach(symbol => {
        subscribe(`market:${symbol}`);
      });
    }

    return () => {
      symbols.forEach(symbol => {
        unsubscribe(`market:${symbol}`);
      });
    };
  }, [isConnected]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (lastMessage && lastMessage.type === 'market_update') {
      const data = lastMessage.data;
      setQuotes(prev => {
        const newQuotes = new Map(prev);
        newQuotes.set(data.symbol, data);
        return newQuotes;
      });
    }
  }, [lastMessage]);

  return (
    <div className="bg-gray-900 text-white py-2 overflow-hidden">
      <div className="flex items-center space-x-8 animate-scroll">
        {Array.from(quotes.values()).map(quote => (
          <div key={quote.symbol} className="flex items-center space-x-3 whitespace-nowrap">
            <span className="font-semibold">{quote.symbol}</span>
            <span className="text-lg">{quote.price.toFixed(2)}</span>
            <span className={`text-sm ${quote.change >= 0 ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400'}`}>
              {quote.change >= 0 ? '▲' : '▼'} {Math.abs(quote.change_percent).toFixed(2)}%
            </span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      
      {/* 接続状態インジケーター */}
      <div className="absolute top-2 right-2">
        <div className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${isConnected ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-red-500'}`}></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

8.1 ログ管理
8.1.1 構造化ログ実装
backend/core/logging_config.py
"""
構造化ログ設定
JSON形式でログを出力し、検索・分析を容易にする
"""
import logging
import json
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Dict
import sys

class JSONFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    """JSON形式のログフォーマッター"""
    
    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        log_data: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
            "level": record.levelname,
            "logger": record.name,
            "message": record.getMessage(),
            "module": record.module,
            "function": record.funcName,
            "line": record.lineno,
        }
        
        # 例外情報
        if record.exc_info:
            log_data["exception"] = self.formatException(record.exc_info)
        
        # 追加フィールド
        if hasattr(record, "user_id"):
            log_data["user_id"] = record.user_id
        
        if hasattr(record, "request_id"):
            log_data["request_id"] = record.request_id
        
        if hasattr(record, "duration_ms"):
            log_data["duration_ms"] = record.duration_ms
        
        return json.dumps(log_data)

def setup_logging(log_level: str = "INFO"):
    """ログ設定をセットアップ"""
    
    # ルートロガー
    root_logger = logging.getLogger()
    root_logger.setLevel(getattr(logging, log_level.upper()))
    
    # ハンドラー削除（既存）
    for handler in root_logger.handlers[:]:
        root_logger.removeHandler(handler)
    
    # コンソールハンドラー
    console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    console_handler.setFormatter(JSONFormatter())
    root_logger.addHandler(console_handler)
    
    # ファイルハンドラー（オプション）
    file_handler = logging.FileHandler("/var/log/economic-platform/app.log")
    file_handler.setFormatter(JSONFormatter())
    root_logger.addHandler(file_handler)
    
    # 外部ライブラリのログレベル調整
    logging.getLogger("uvicorn").setLevel(logging.WARNING)
    logging.getLogger("fastapi").setLevel(logging.WARNING)
    logging.getLogger("sqlalchemy").setLevel(logging.WARNING)
    
    return root_logger

# アプリケーション起動時に呼び出し
logger = setup_logging()

backend/main.py（ログ統合）
from backend.core.logging_config import setup_logging
import logging
import time
import uuid

# ログ設定
logger = setup_logging(log_level=settings.LOG_LEVEL)

# リクエストIDミドルウェア
@app.middleware("http")
async def add_request_id_and_logging(request, call_next):
    request_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    request.state.request_id = request_id
    
    start_time = time.time()
    
    # リクエストログ
    logger.info(
        f"Request started",
        extra={
            "request_id": request_id,
            "method": request.method,
            "url": str(request.url),
            "client_ip": request.client.host
        }
    )
    
    try:
        response = await call_next(request)
        
        duration_ms = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
        
        # レスポンスログ
        logger.info(
            f"Request completed",
            extra={
                "request_id": request_id,
                "method": request.method,
                "url": str(request.url),
                "status_code": response.status_code,
                "duration_ms": duration_ms
            }
        )
        
        response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id
        return response
    
    except Exception as e:
        duration_ms = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
        
        logger.error(
            f"Request failed",
            extra={
                "request_id": request_id,
                "method": request.method,
                "url": str(request.url),
                "duration_ms": duration_ms
            },
            exc_info=True
        )
        raise
